            </div>
            <div class="users-list" id="users">
                <div class="loading" id="usersLoading">
                    <div class="spinner"></div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Server Actions -->
        <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
                <i class="fas fa-tools"></i>
                <h3>Actions serveur</h3>
            </div>
            <div class="button-group">
                <button class="btn btn-danger" id="btnRestartServer" disabled>
                    <i class="fas fa-redo"></i>
                    Redémarrer le serveur
                </button>
                <button class="btn btn-warning" id="btnClearAll" disabled>
                    <i class="fas fa-broom"></i>
                    Nettoyer tout
                </button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="btnExportData" disabled>
                    <i class="fas fa-file-export"></i>
                    Exporter les données
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        // Configuration
        const CONFIG = {
            SERVER_IP: localStorage.getItem('admin_server_ip') || '127.0.0.1',
            SERVER_PORT: localStorage.getItem('admin_server_port') || '8000',
            AUTO_REFRESH: true,
            REFRESH_INTERVAL: 3000
        };

        // État de l'application
        const STATE = {
            serverUrl: null,
            isConnected: false,
            users: {},
            stats: {
                totalUsers: 0,
                onlineUsers: 0,
                totalMessages: 0,
                totalFiles: 0
            },
            serverConfig: {
                min_username_length: 3,
                max_username_length: 20,
                min_password_length: 6,
                max_password_length: 50
            },
            pendingAction: null,
            refreshInterval: null,
            visiblePasswords: {},
            selectedUserForColor: null,
            colorPresets: [
                '#6366f1', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#ef4444', '#8b5cf6', '#ec4899',
                '#06b6d4', '#84cc16', '#f97316', '#64748b', '#0ea5e9', '#a855f7',
                '#d946ef', '#f43f5e', '#eab308', '#22c55e', '#3b82f6', '#14b8a6'
            ]
        };

        // Éléments DOM
        const DOM = {
            // Configuration
            minUsernameLength: document.getElementById('minUsernameLength'),
            maxUsernameLength: document.getElementById('maxUsernameLength'),
            minPasswordLength: document.getElementById('minPasswordLength'),
            maxPasswordLength: document.getElementById('maxPasswordLength'),
            saveConfigBtn: document.getElementById('saveConfigBtn'),
            
            // Connexion serveur
            serverIP: document.getElementById('serverIP'),
            serverPort: document.getElementById('serverPort'),
            btnStart: document.getElementById('btnStart'),
            btnStop: document.getElementById('btnStop'),
            btnClearChat: document.getElementById('btnClearChat'),
            btnBackup: document.getElementById('btnBackup'),
            status: document.getElementById('status'),
            stats: document.getElementById('stats'),
            
            // Statistiques
            totalUsers: document.getElementById('totalUsers'),
            onlineUsers: document.getElementById('onlineUsers'),
            totalMessages: document.getElementById('totalMessages'),
            totalFiles: document.getElementById('totalFiles'),
            
            // Création utilisateur
            newUser: document.getElementById('newUser'),
            newPass: document.getElementById('newPass'),
            userRole: document.getElementById('userRole'),
            userColorPreview: document.getElementById('userColorPreview'),
            userColorInput: document.getElementById('userColorInput'),
            btnCreateUser: document.getElementById('btnCreateUser'),
            
            // Liste utilisateurs
            users: document.getElementById('users'),
            usersLoading: document.getElementById('usersLoading'),
            usersCount: document.getElementById('usersCount'),
            
            // Actions serveur
            btnRestartServer: document.getElementById('btnRestartServer'),
            btnClearAll: document.getElementById('btnClearAll'),
            btnExportData: document.getElementById('btnExportData'),
            
            // Modals
            confirmModal: document.getElementById('confirmModal'),
            confirmMessage: document.getElementById('confirmMessage'),
            closeConfirmModal: document.getElementById('closeConfirmModal'),
            cancelConfirmBtn: document.getElementById('cancelConfirmBtn'),
            confirmActionBtn: document.getElementById('confirmActionBtn'),
            
            colorModal: document.getElementById('colorModal'),
            colorPreviewLarge: document.getElementById('colorPreviewLarge'),
            colorInputLarge: document.getElementById('colorInputLarge'),
            colorPresets: document.getElementById('colorPresets'),
            saveColorBtn: document.getElementById('saveColorBtn'),
            selectedUserInfo: document.getElementById('selectedUserInfo'),
            
            // Toast
            toastContainer: document.getElementById('toastContainer')
        };

        // Initialisation
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);

        function init() {
            // Charger la configuration
            DOM.serverIP.value = CONFIG.SERVER_IP;
            DOM.serverPort.value = CONFIG.SERVER_PORT;
            
            // Configurer les événements
            setupEventListeners();
            
            // Configurer les presets de couleur
            setupColorPresets();
            
            // Tester la connexion au démarrage
            testConnection();
        }

        // Configurer les événements
        function setupEventListeners() {
            // Configuration système
            DOM.saveConfigBtn.addEventListener('click', saveSystemConfig);
            
            // Connexion/Déconnexion
            DOM.btnStart.addEventListener('click', connectServer);
            DOM.btnStop.addEventListener('click', disconnectServer);
            DOM.btnClearChat.addEventListener('click', () => showConfirm('clearChat'));
            DOM.btnBackup.addEventListener('click', createBackup);
            
            // Création utilisateur
            DOM.btnCreateUser.addEventListener('click', createUser);
            DOM.newUser.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') DOM.newPass.focus();
            });
            DOM.newPass.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') createUser();
            });
            DOM.userColorInput.addEventListener('input', updateUserColorPreview);
            DOM.userColorInput.addEventListener('change', validateColor);
            
            // Actions serveur
            DOM.btnRestartServer.addEventListener('click', () => showConfirm('restart'));
            DOM.btnClearAll.addEventListener('click', () => showConfirm('clearAll'));
            DOM.btnExportData.addEventListener('click', exportData);
            
            // Modal confirmation
            DOM.closeConfirmModal.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmModal);
            DOM.cancelConfirmBtn.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmModal);
            DOM.confirmActionBtn.addEventListener('click', executePendingAction);
            
            // Modal couleur
            DOM.colorInputLarge.addEventListener('input', updateLargeColorPreview);
            DOM.saveColorBtn.addEventListener('click', saveUserColor);
            
            // Connexion avec Entrée
            DOM.serverIP.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') connectServer();
            });
            DOM.serverPort.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') connectServer();
            });
        }

        // Configurer les presets de couleur
        function setupColorPresets() {
            DOM.colorPresets.innerHTML = '';
            STATE.colorPresets.forEach(color => {
                const preset = document.createElement('div');
                preset.className = 'color-preset';
                preset.style.backgroundColor = color;
                preset.title = color;
                preset.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    DOM.colorInputLarge.value = color;
                    updateLargeColorPreview();
                });
                DOM.colorPresets.appendChild(preset);
            });
        }

        // Générer une couleur aléatoire
        function generateRandomColor() {
            const letters = '0123456789ABCDEF';
            let color = '#';
            for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                color += letters[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
            }
            DOM.colorInputLarge.value = color;
            updateLargeColorPreview();
        }

        // Générer une couleur aléatoire pour nouvel utilisateur
        function generateRandomUserColor() {
            const letters = '0123456789ABCDEF';
            let color = '#';
            for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                color += letters[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
            }
            DOM.userColorInput.value = color;
            updateUserColorPreview();
        }

        // Mettre à jour l'aperçu de couleur
        function updateUserColorPreview() {
            DOM.userColorPreview.style.backgroundColor = DOM.userColorInput.value;
        }

        // Mettre à jour l'aperçu de couleur large
        function updateLargeColorPreview() {
            DOM.colorPreviewLarge.style.backgroundColor = DOM.colorInputLarge.value;
        }

        // Valider une couleur
        function validateColor(input) {
            const color = input.value;
            if (!/^#[0-9A-F]{6}$/i.test(color)) {
                input.style.borderColor = 'var(--danger)';
                return false;
            } else {
                input.style.borderColor = 'var(--border)';
                return true;
            }
        }

        // Tester la connexion
        async function testConnection() {
            const serverUrl = `http://${CONFIG.SERVER_IP}:${CONFIG.SERVER_PORT}`;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${serverUrl}/health`, {
                    method: 'GET',
                    mode: 'cors',
                    timeout: 3000
                }).catch(() => null);
                
                if (response && response.ok) {
                    updateStatus(true, 'Serveur accessible');
                    return true;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                // Ignorer les erreurs de test
            }
            
            return false;
        }

        // Connecter au serveur
        async function connectServer() {
            const ip = DOM.serverIP.value.trim();
            const port = DOM.serverPort.value.trim();
            
            if (!ip) {
                showToast('Veuillez entrer une adresse IP', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            if (!port || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
                showToast('Port invalide', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            // Sauvegarder la configuration
            localStorage.setItem('admin_server_ip', ip);
            localStorage.setItem('admin_server_port', port);
            
            CONFIG.SERVER_IP = ip;
            CONFIG.SERVER_PORT = port;
            
            STATE.serverUrl = `http://${ip}:${port}`;
            
            // Afficher le chargement
            DOM.btnStart.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Connexion...';
            DOM.btnStart.disabled = true;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/users`, {
                    method: 'GET',
                    mode: 'cors',
                    headers: {
                        'Accept': 'application/json'
                    }
                });
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    updateStatus(true);
                    
                    // Charger la configuration du serveur
                    await loadServerConfig();
                    
                    // Charger les données
                    await Promise.all([
                        loadUsers(),
                        loadStats()
                    ]);
                    
                    // Activer le rafraîchissement automatique
                    startAutoRefresh();
                    
                    showToast('Connecté au serveur', 'success');
                    
                } else {
                    throw new Error('Serveur inaccessible');
                }
                
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erreur connexion:', error);
                showToast(
                    'Impossible de se connecter au serveur. Vérifiez:\n1. L\'adresse IP est correcte\n2. Le serveur est en cours d\'exécution\n3. Le port est accessible',
                    'error'
                );
                updateStatus(false);
            } finally {
                DOM.btnStart.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-plug"></i> Se connecter';
                DOM.btnStart.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        // Charger la configuration du serveur
        async function loadServerConfig() {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) return;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/config`);
                if (response.ok) {
                    const config = await response.json();
                    STATE.serverConfig = config;
                    
                    // Mettre à jour l'interface
                    DOM.minUsernameLength.value = config.min_username_length || 3;
                    DOM.maxUsernameLength.value = config.max_username_length || 20;
                    DOM.minPasswordLength.value = config.min_password_length || 6;
                    DOM.maxPasswordLength.value = config.max_password_length || 50;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erreur chargement config:', error);
            }
        }

        // Sauvegarder la configuration système
        async function saveSystemConfig() {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) {
                showToast('Veuillez d\'abord vous connecter au serveur', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            const config = {
                min_username_length: parseInt(DOM.minUsernameLength.value),
                max_username_length: parseInt(DOM.maxUsernameLength.value),
                min_password_length: parseInt(DOM.minPasswordLength.value),
                max_password_length: parseInt(DOM.maxPasswordLength.value)
            };
            
            // Validation
            if (config.min_username_length < 1 || config.min_username_length > 50) {
                showToast('Longueur min nom d\'utilisateur invalide (1-50)', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            if (config.max_username_length < 1 || config.max_username_length > 100) {
                showToast('Longueur max nom d\'utilisateur invalide (1-100)', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            if (config.min_username_length > config.max_username_length) {
                showToast('Le min ne peut pas être supérieur au max', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            if (config.min_password_length < 1 || config.min_password_length > 100) {
                showToast('Longueur min mot de passe invalide (1-100)', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            if (config.max_password_length < 1 || config.max_password_length > 200) {
                showToast('Longueur max mot de passe invalide (1-200)', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            if (config.min_password_length > config.max_password_length) {
                showToast('Le min ne peut pas être supérieur au max', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/update_config`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        username: 'admin',
                        ...config
                    })
                });
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    STATE.serverConfig = config;
                    showToast('Configuration sauvegardée avec succès', 'success');
                } else {
                    const error = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(error.detail || 'Erreur de sauvegarde');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showToast(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        // Déconnecter du serveur
        function disconnectServer() {
            updateStatus(false);
            stopAutoRefresh();
            
            // Réinitialiser les données
            STATE.users = {};
            STATE.stats = {
                totalUsers: 0,
                onlineUsers: 0,
                totalMessages: 0,
                totalFiles: 0
            };
            STATE.visiblePasswords = {};
            
            // Réinitialiser l'interface
            DOM.users.innerHTML = '';
            DOM.usersLoading.style.display = 'flex';
            DOM.usersCount.textContent = '0';
            
            showToast('Déconnecté du serveur', 'warning');
        }

        // Mettre à jour le statut
        function updateStatus(connected, message = '') {
            STATE.isConnected = connected;
            
            const status = DOM.status;
            const stats = DOM.stats;
            
            if (connected) {
                status.className = 'status status-connected';
                status.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i><span>Statut : Connecté au serveur${message ? ` - ${message}` : ''}</span>`;
                stats.style.display = 'grid';
                
                DOM.btnStart.disabled = true;
                DOM.btnStop.disabled = false;
                DOM.btnClearChat.disabled = false;
                DOM.btnBackup.disabled = false;
                DOM.btnRestartServer.disabled = false;
                DOM.btnClearAll.disabled = false;
                DOM.btnExportData.disabled = false;
                DOM.saveConfigBtn.disabled = false;
                
            } else {
                status.className = 'status status-disconnected';
                status.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i><span>Statut : Déconnecté${message ? ` - ${message}` : ''}</span>`;
                stats.style.display = 'none';
                
                DOM.btnStart.disabled = false;
                DOM.btnStop.disabled = true;
                DOM.btnClearChat.disabled = true;
                DOM.btnBackup.disabled = true;
                DOM.btnRestartServer.disabled = true;
                DOM.btnClearAll.disabled = true;
                DOM.btnExportData.disabled = true;
                DOM.saveConfigBtn.disabled = true;
            }
        }

        // Charger les utilisateurs
        async function loadUsers() {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) return;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/users`);
                if (response.ok) {
                    const users = await response.json();
                    STATE.users = users;
                    renderUsers(users);
                    
                    DOM.usersLoading.style.display = 'none';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erreur chargement utilisateurs:', error);
            }
        }

        // Afficher les utilisateurs
        function renderUsers(users) {
            const usersDiv = DOM.users;
            usersDiv.innerHTML = '';
            
            const userList = Object.entries(users);
            
            if (userList.length === 0) {
                usersDiv.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="empty-state">
                        <i class="fas fa-users-slash"></i>
                        <h4>Aucun utilisateur</h4>
                        <p>Créez votre premier utilisateur</p>
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }
            
            userList.forEach(([username, userData]) => {
                const isOnline = userData.online === true;
                const isAdmin = userData.role === 'admin';
                const createdDate = userData.created_at ? formatDate(userData.created_at) : 'Inconnue';
                const lastLogin = userData.last_login ? formatDate(userData.last_login) : 'Jamais';
                const userColor = userData.color || '#6366f1';
                
                const userElement = document.createElement('div');
                userElement.className = 'user-item';
                userElement.dataset.username = username;
                
                userElement.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="user-info">
                        <div class="user-avatar" style="background: ${userColor};">
                            ${username.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                            ${isAdmin ? '<div class="admin-badge"><i class="fas fa-crown"></i></div>' : ''}
                        </div>
                        <div class="user-details">
                            <div class="user-name" style="color: ${userColor}">
                                ${escapeHtml(username)}
                                ${isAdmin ? '<span style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.75rem; background: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.1); padding: 0.125rem 0.5rem; border-radius: var(--radius-full);">Admin</span>' : ''}
                            </div>
                            <div class="user-status ${isOnline ? 'online' : 'offline'}">
                                <i class="fas fa-circle"></i>
                                ${isOnline ? 'En ligne' : 'Hors ligne'}
                            </div>
                            <div class="user-meta">
                                Créé: ${createdDate} | Dernière connexion: ${lastLogin}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="user-actions">
                        <div class="password-display" id="password-${username}">
                            <div class="password-label">Mot de passe</div>
                            <div class="password-text">${userData.password}</div>
                        </div>
                        <button class="user-action-btn password" onclick="togglePassword('${username}')" title="Voir le mot de passe">
                            <i class="fas fa-eye"></i>
                        </button>
                        <button class="user-action-btn color" onclick="openColorModal('${username}')" title="Modifier la couleur">
                            <i class="fas fa-palette"></i>
                        </button>
                        ${isOnline ? `
                            <button class="user-action-btn logout" onclick="forceLogout('${username}')" title="Déconnecter cet utilisateur">
                                <i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i>
                            </button>
                        ` : ''}
                        <button class="user-action-btn delete" onclick="deleteUser('${username}')" title="Supprimer cet utilisateur">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;
                
                usersDiv.appendChild(userElement);
            });
            
            DOM.usersCount.textContent = userList.length;
        }

        // Basculer l'affichage du mot de passe
        function togglePassword(username) {
            const passwordElement = document.getElementById(`password-${username}`);
            
            if (passwordElement.classList.contains('show')) {
                passwordElement.classList.remove('show');
            } else {
                // Masquer tous les autres mots de passe
                document.querySelectorAll('.password-display.show').forEach(el => {
                    el.classList.remove('show');
                });
                
                // Afficher celui-ci
                passwordElement.classList.add('show');
                
                // Masquer automatiquement après 5 secondes
                setTimeout(() => {
                    passwordElement.classList.remove('show');
                }, 5000);
            }
        }

        // Ouvrir le modal de modification de couleur
        function openColorModal(username) {
            if (!STATE.users[username]) return;
            
            STATE.selectedUserForColor = username;
            const userData = STATE.users[username];
            const currentColor = userData.color || '#6366f1';
            
            DOM.colorInputLarge.value = currentColor;
            DOM.colorPreviewLarge.style.backgroundColor = currentColor;
            
            DOM.selectedUserInfo.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--text-2);">
                    Modification de la couleur pour: <strong style="color: ${currentColor}">${username}</strong>
                </div>
            `;
            
            DOM.colorModal.style.display = 'flex';
        }

        // Fermer le modal de couleur
        function closeColorModal() {
            DOM.colorModal.style.display = 'none';
            STATE.selectedUserForColor = null;
        }

        // Sauvegarder la couleur d'un utilisateur
        async function saveUserColor() {
            const username = STATE.selectedUserForColor;
            const color = DOM.colorInputLarge.value.trim();
            
            if (!username || !color) return;
            
            if (!/^#[0-9A-F]{6}$/i.test(color)) {
                showToast('Couleur invalide. Format: #RRGGBB', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/update_user_color`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        username: username,
                        color: color,
                        admin_username: 'admin'
                    })
                });
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    // Mettre à jour localement
                    if (STATE.users[username]) {
                        STATE.users[username].color = color;
                        renderUsers(STATE.users);
                    }
                    
                    closeColorModal();
                    showToast(`Couleur de ${username} mise à jour`, 'success');
                } else {
                    const error = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(error.detail);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showToast(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        // Charger les statistiques
        async function loadStats() {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) return;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/stats`);
                if (response.ok) {
                    const stats = await response.json();
                    STATE.stats = stats;
                    updateStatsDisplay(stats);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erreur chargement stats:', error);
            }
        }

        // Mettre à jour l'affichage des statistiques
        function updateStatsDisplay(stats) {
            DOM.totalUsers.textContent = stats.totalUsers || 0;
            DOM.onlineUsers.textContent = stats.onlineUsers || 0;
            DOM.totalMessages.textContent = stats.totalMessages || 0;
            DOM.totalFiles.textContent = stats.totalFiles || 0;
        }

        // Créer un utilisateur
        async function createUser() {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) {
                showToast('Veuillez d\'abord vous connecter au serveur', 'error');
                return;
            }

            const username = DOM.newUser.value.trim();
            const password = DOM.newPass.value.trim();
            const role = DOM.userRole.value;
            const color = DOM.userColorInput.value.trim();

            // Validation avec config dynamique
            if (username.length < STATE.serverConfig.min_username_length) {
                showToast(`Le nom d'utilisateur doit contenir au moins ${STATE.serverConfig.min_username_length} caractères`, 'error');
                DOM.newUser.focus();
                return;
            }

            if (username.length > STATE.serverConfig.max_username_length) {
                showToast(`Le nom d'utilisateur ne peut pas dépasser ${STATE.serverConfig.max_username_length} caractères`, 'error');
                DOM.newUser.focus();
                return;
            }

            if (password.length < STATE.serverConfig.min_password_length) {
                showToast(`Le mot de passe doit contenir au moins ${STATE.serverConfig.min_password_length} caractères`, 'error');
                DOM.newPass.focus();
                return;
            }

            if (password.length > STATE.serverConfig.max_password_length) {
                showToast(`Le mot de passe ne peut pas dépasser ${STATE.serverConfig.max_password_length} caractères`, 'error');
                DOM.newPass.focus();
                return;
            }

            if (!/^#[0-9A-F]{6}$/i.test(color)) {
                showToast('Couleur invalide. Format: #RRGGBB', 'error');
                return;
            }

            // Vérifier si l'utilisateur existe déjà
            if (STATE.users[username]) {
                showToast('Cet utilisateur existe déjà', 'error');
                return;
            }

            // Désactiver le bouton
            DOM.btnCreateUser.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Création...';
            DOM.btnCreateUser.disabled = true;

            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/create_user`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ username, password, role, color })
                });

                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    showToast(`Utilisateur "${username}" créé avec succès`, 'success');
                    
                    // Réinitialiser le formulaire
                    DOM.newUser.value = '';
                    DOM.newPass.value = '';
                    DOM.userColorInput.value = '#6366f1';
                    DOM.userColorPreview.style.backgroundColor = '#6366f1';
                    DOM.userRole.value = 'user';
                    
                    // Recharger les données
                    await Promise.all([
                        loadUsers(),
                        loadStats()
                    ]);
                    
                    DOM.newUser.focus();
                    
                } else {
                    throw new Error(data.detail || 'Erreur lors de la création');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showToast(error.message, 'error');
            } finally {
                DOM.btnCreateUser.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-user-plus"></i> Créer l\'utilisateur';
                DOM.btnCreateUser.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        // Forcer la déconnexion d'un utilisateur - ACTION INSTANTANÉE
        async function forceLogout(username) {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) return;
            
            // Mise à jour UI immédiate
            const userElement = document.querySelector(`[data-username="${username}"]`);
            if (userElement) {
                const statusElement = userElement.querySelector('.user-status');
                if (statusElement) {
                    statusElement.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-circle"></i> Déconnexion...';
                    statusElement.className = 'user-status offline';
                }
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/force_logout/${username}`, {
                    method: 'POST'
                });
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    showToast(`Utilisateur "${username}" déconnecté`, 'success');
                    
                    // Recharger les données
                    await Promise.all([
                        loadUsers(),
                        loadStats()
                    ]);
                    
                } else {
                    const data = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(data.detail);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showToast(error.message, 'error');
                // Recharger pour avoir l'état correct
                await loadUsers();
            }
        }

        // Supprimer un utilisateur - ACTION INSTANTANÉE
        async function deleteUser(username) {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) return;
            
            // Confirmation rapide
            if (!confirm(`Supprimer l'utilisateur "${username}" ?\n\nCette action est irréversible !`)) {
                return;
            }
            
            // Mise à jour UI immédiate
            const userElement = document.querySelector(`[data-username="${username}"]`);
            if (userElement) {
                userElement.style.opacity = '0.5';
                userElement.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                
                // Ajouter un indicateur de suppression
                const actionsDiv = userElement.querySelector('.user-actions');
                if (actionsDiv) {
                    actionsDiv.innerHTML = '<span style="color: var(--text-3); font-size: 0.75rem;">Suppression...</span>';
                }
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/delete_user`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ 
                        username: username,
                        admin_username: 'admin' 
                    })
                });
                
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (response.ok) {
                    // Supprimer l'élément immédiatement
                    if (userElement) {
                        userElement.remove();
                    }
                    
                    // Mettre à jour le compteur
                    const count = parseInt(DOM.usersCount.textContent) - 1;
                    DOM.usersCount.textContent = count;
                    
                    // Mettre à jour les stats
                    await loadStats();
                    
                    showToast(`Utilisateur "${username}" supprimé`, 'success');
                    
                } else {
                    throw new Error(data.detail || 'Erreur de suppression');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showToast(error.message, 'error');
                // Recharger pour avoir l'état correct
                await loadUsers();
            }
        }

        // Exporter les données
        async function exportData() {
            if (!STATE.isConnected) return;
            
            try {
                const [usersResponse, messagesResponse, configResponse] = await Promise.all([
                    fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/users`),
                    fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/messages`),
                    fetch(`${STATE.serverUrl}/config`)
                ]);
                
                const [users, messages, config] = await Promise.all([
                    usersResponse.json(),
                    messagesResponse.json(),
                    configResponse.json()
                ]);
                
                const exportData = {
                    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
                    server: STATE.serverUrl,
                    config: config,
                    users: users,
                    messages: messages,
                    stats: STATE.stats
                };
                
                const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2)], {
                    type: 'application/json'
                });
                
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                const a = document.createElement('a');
                a.href = url;
                a.download = `messagerie_export_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
                document.body.appendChild(a);
                a.click();
                document.body.removeChild(a);
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                
                showToast('Données exportées avec succès', 'success');
                
            } catch (error) {
                showToast('Erreur lors de l\'exportation', 'error');
            }
        }

        // Démarrer le rafraîchissement automatique
        function startAutoRefresh() {
            if (STATE.refreshInterval) {
                clearInterval(STATE.refreshInterval);
            }
            
            STATE.refreshInterval = setInterval(() => {
                if (STATE.isConnected) {
                    Promise.all([
                        loadUsers(),
                        loadStats()  // AJOUT IMPORTANT: Actualiser les stats aussi
                    ]).catch(() => {
                        // En cas d'erreur, arrêter le rafraîchissement
                        stopAutoRefresh();
                        updateStatus(false, 'Connexion perdue');
                    });
                }
            }, CONFIG.REFRESH_INTERVAL);
        }

        // Arrêter le rafraîchissement automatique
        function stopAutoRefresh() {
            if (STATE.refreshInterval) {
                clearInterval(STATE.refreshInterval);
                STATE.refreshInterval = null;
            }
        }

        // Afficher le modal de confirmation
        function showConfirm(action) {
            let message = '';
            let confirmText = 'Confirmer';
            
            switch (action) {
                case 'clearChat':
                    message = '⚠️ ATTENTION : Action irréversible\n\nVoulez-vous vraiment supprimer TOUT l\'historique du chat ?\n\nCette action supprimera tous les messages de tous les utilisateurs.';
                    confirmText = 'Supprimer l\'historique';
                    break;
                    
                case 'restart':
                    message = 'Redémarrer le serveur ?\n\nTous les utilisateurs seront déconnectés temporairement.';
                    confirmText = 'Redémarrer';
                    break;
                    
                case 'clearAll':
                    message = '⚠️ ACTION DANGEREUSE\n\nVoulez-vous vraiment supprimer TOUTES les données ?\n\nCela inclut :\n• Tous les utilisateurs (y compris les admins)\n• Tous les messages\n• Tous les fichiers\n\nCette action est irréversible !';
                    confirmText = 'Tout supprimer';
                    break;
            }
            
            STATE.pendingAction = action;
            DOM.confirmMessage.textContent = message;
            DOM.confirmActionBtn.textContent = confirmText;
            DOM.confirmModal.style.display = 'flex';
        }

        // Fermer le modal de confirmation
        function closeConfirmModal() {
            DOM.confirmModal.style.display = 'none';
            STATE.pendingAction = null;
        }

        // Exécuter l'action en attente
        async function executePendingAction() {
            const action = STATE.pendingAction;
            closeConfirmModal();
            
            switch (action) {
                case 'clearChat':
                    await clearChatHistory();
                    break;
                    
                case 'restart':
                    await restartServer();
                    break;
                    
                case 'clearAll':
                    await clearAll();
                    break;
            }
        }

        // Afficher un toast
        function showToast(message, type = 'info', duration = 5000) {
            const toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.className = `toast ${type}`;
            toast.innerHTML = `
                <div class="toast-content">
                    <div class="toast-title">${type === 'success' ? 'Succès' : type === 'error' ? 'Erreur' : type === 'warning' ? 'Attention' : 'Information'}</div>
                    <div class="toast-message">${message}</div>
                </div>
                <button class="toast-close" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">
                    <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
                </button>
            `;
            
            DOM.toastContainer.appendChild(toast);
            
            // Supprimer automatiquement
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (toast.parentElement) {
                    toast.remove();
                }
            }, duration);
            
            // Supprimer après un clic
            toast.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                if (e.target.closest('.toast-close')) return;
                this.remove();
            });
        }

        // Formater la date
        function formatDate(dateString) {
            try {
                const date = new Date(dateString);
                const now = new Date();
                const diffMs = now - date;
                const diffMins = Math.floor(diffMs / 60000);
                const diffHours = Math.floor(diffMs / 3600000);
                const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / 86400000);
                
                if (diffMins < 1) return 'à l\'instant';
                if (diffMins < 60) return `il y a ${diffMins} min`;
                if (diffHours < 24) return `il y a ${diffHours} h`;
                if (diffDays < 7) return `il y a ${diffDays} j`;
                
                return date.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
                    day: 'numeric',
                    month: 'short',
                    year: 'numeric'
                });
            } catch {
                return dateString;
            }
        }

        // Échapper le HTML
        function escapeHtml(text) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.textContent = text;
            return div.innerHTML;
        }

        // Générer une couleur aléatoire (fonction utilitaire)
        function generateRandomColor() {
            const letters = '0123456789ABCDEF';
            let color = '#';
            for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                color += letters[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)];
            }
            return color;
        }

        // Exposer les fonctions globales
        window.forceLogout = forceLogout;
        window.deleteUser = deleteUser;
        window.togglePassword = togglePassword;
        window.openColorModal = openColorModal;
        window.closeColorModal = closeColorModal;
        window.generateRandomColor = generateRandomColor;
        window.generateRandomUserColor = generateRandomUserColor;
    </script>

# Configuration simple

app = FastAPI(title="Serveur de Messagerie", version="2.1")

# CORS - Configuration améliorée
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,

BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
DATA_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "data")
UPLOADS_DIR = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "uploads")

os.makedirs(DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(UPLOADS_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# Configuration globale
CONFIG = {
    "min_username_length": 3,
    "max_username_length": 50,  # Augmenté
    "min_password_length": 1,   # Réduit (aucune limite pratique)
    "max_password_length": 500,  # Augmenté
    "default_user_color": "#6366f1",
    "max_message_length": 10000000000000,  # Longueur max des messages augmentée
    "allow_all_file_types": True  # Nouveau: autoriser tous les types
}

# Charger la configuration
def load_config():
    global CONFIG
    try:
        if os.path.exists(CONFIG_FILE):
            with open(CONFIG_FILE, 'r') as f:
                saved_config = json.load(f)
                CONFIG.update(saved_config)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erreur chargement config: {e}")
    return CONFIG

def save_config():
    try:
        with open(CONFIG_FILE, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(CONFIG, f, indent=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erreur sauvegarde config: {e}")

# Charger les données
def load_data():
    try:
        if os.path.exists(USERS_FILE):
            with open(USERS_FILE, 'r') as f:
                users = json.load(f)
        else:
            users = {}
            
        if os.path.exists(MESSAGES_FILE):
            with open(MESSAGES_FILE, 'r') as f:
                messages = json.load(f)
        else:
            messages = {"messages": [], "last_id": 0}
            
        return users, messages
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erreur chargement données: {e}")
        return {}, {"messages": [], "last_id": 0}

def save_data(users, messages):
    try:
        with open(USERS_FILE, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(users, f, indent=2)
        with open(MESSAGES_FILE, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(messages, f, indent=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erreur sauvegarde: {e}")

# Générer une couleur aléatoire
def generate_random_color():
    return f"#{secrets.token_hex(3)}"

# Charger les données initiales
users, messages = load_data()
load_config()

# Gestion WebSocket
class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: Dict[str, WebSocket] = {}
        self.user_colors = {}
    
    async def connect(self, websocket: WebSocket, username: str):
        await websocket.accept()
        self.active_connections[username] = websocket
        users[username]["online"] = True
        save_data(users, messages)
        await self.broadcast_status()
    
    def disconnect(self, username: str):
        if username in self.active_connections:
            del self.active_connections[username]
        if username in users:
            users[username]["online"] = False
            save_data(users, messages)
    
    async def send_message(self, message: dict, username: str):
        if username in self.active_connections:
            try:
                await self.active_connections[username].send_json(message)
            except:
                self.disconnect(username)
    
    async def broadcast(self, message: dict):
        for username, websocket in list(self.active_connections.items()):
            try:
                await websocket.send_json(message)
            except:
                self.disconnect(username)
    
    async def broadcast_status(self):
        online_users = list(self.active_connections.keys())
        status_message = {
            "type": "user_status",
            "online_users": online_users,
            "count": len(online_users)
        }
        await self.broadcast(status_message)
    
    def get_user_color(self, username: str):
        if username in users and "color" in users[username]:
            return users[username]["color"]
        # Générer une couleur si elle n'existe pas
        color = generate_random_color()
        if username in users:
            users[username]["color"] = color
            save_data(users, messages)
        return color
    
    def set_user_color(self, username: str, color: str):
        if username in users:
            users[username]["color"] = color
            save_data(users, messages)

manager = ConnectionManager()

    }

@app.post("/update_config")
async def update_config(data: dict):
    username = data.get("username")
    
    if username not in users or users[username].get("role") != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Accès refusé")
    
    # Mettre à jour la configuration
    for key, value in data.items():
        if key in CONFIG and key not in ["username", "admin_key"]:
            CONFIG[key] = value
    
    save_config()
    
    return {"detail": "Configuration mise à jour", "config": CONFIG}

# WebSocket
@app.websocket("/ws/{username}")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket, username: str):
    if username not in users:
        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason="Utilisateur inconnu")
        return
    
    await manager.connect(websocket, username)
    
    try:
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            try:
                message = json.loads(data)
                if message.get("type") == "ping":
                    await websocket.send_json({"type": "pong"})
                elif message.get("type") == "typing":
                    await manager.broadcast({
                        "type": "user_typing",
                        "username": username,
                        "is_typing": message.get("is_typing", False)
                    })
            except:
                pass
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(username)
        await manager.broadcast_status()

# Authentification
@app.post("/login")
async def login(data: dict):
    username = data.get("username", "").strip()
    password = data.get("password", "").strip()

    if not username or not password:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Champs requis")
    
    if username not in users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Utilisateur inconnu")
    
    if users[username]["password"] != password:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Mot de passe incorrect")
    
    users[username]["online"] = True
    users[username]["last_login"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    save_data(users, messages)
    
    logger.info(f"✅ {username} connecté")
    
    return {
        "detail": "Connecté",
        "username": username,
        "online": True,
        "color": manager.get_user_color(username)
    }

@app.post("/logout")
async def logout(data: dict):
    username = data.get("username", "").strip()
    
    if username in users:
        users[username]["online"] = False
        users[username]["last_logout"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
        save_data(users, messages)
        manager.disconnect(username)
        await manager.broadcast_status()
    
    return {"detail": "Déconnecté"}

# Utilisateurs
@app.get("/users")
async def get_users():
    # Mettre à jour les statuts
    for username in users:
        users[username]["online"] = username in manager.active_connections
        # Ajouter la couleur si elle n'existe pas
        if "color" not in users[username]:
            users[username]["color"] = manager.get_user_color(username)
    save_data(users, messages)
    
    # Formater la réponse avec les couleurs
    formatted_users = {}
    for username, data in users.items():
        formatted_users[username] = {
            **data,
            "color": manager.get_user_color(username)
        }
    
    return formatted_users

@app.get("/user/{username}")
async def get_user(username: str):
    if username not in users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Utilisateur introuvable")
    
    user_data = users[username].copy()
    user_data["color"] = manager.get_user_color(username)
    
    return user_data

@app.post("/create_user")
async def create_user(data: dict):
    username = data.get("username", "").strip()
    password = data.get("password", "").strip()
    role = data.get("role", "user")
    color = data.get("color", generate_random_color())

    # Validation avec config dynamique
    if len(username) < CONFIG["min_username_length"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, 
                          detail=f"Nom trop court (min {CONFIG['min_username_length']} caractères)")
    
    if len(username) > CONFIG["max_username_length"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400,
                          detail=f"Nom trop long (max {CONFIG['max_username_length']} caractères)")
    
    if len(password) < CONFIG["min_password_length"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400,
                          detail=f"Mot de passe trop court (min {CONFIG['min_password_length']} caractères)")
    
    if len(password) > CONFIG["max_password_length"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400,
                          detail=f"Mot de passe trop long (max {CONFIG['max_password_length']} caractères)")
    
    if username in users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Utilisateur existe déjà")
    
    users[username] = {
        "password": password,
        "online": False,
        "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "last_login": None,
        "role": role,
        "color": color
    }
    
    save_data(users, messages)
    logger.info(f"👤 Compte créé: {username}")
    
    return {
        "detail": "Utilisateur créé",
        "username": username,
        "color": color
    }

@app.post("/delete_user")
async def delete_user(data: dict):
    username_to_delete = data.get("username", "").strip()
    admin_username = data.get("admin_username", "").strip()
    
    if admin_username not in users or users[admin_username].get("role") != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Accès refusé")
    
    if username_to_delete not in users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Utilisateur introuvable")
    
    # NE PAS vérifier si c'est un admin - on peut TOUT supprimer
    
    # Supprimer les messages de cet utilisateur
    messages["messages"] = [msg for msg in messages["messages"] if msg.get("sender") != username_to_delete]
    
    # Déconnecter si en ligne
    manager.disconnect(username_to_delete)
    
    # Supprimer l'utilisateur
    del users[username_to_delete]
    
    save_data(users, messages)
    await manager.broadcast_status()
    
    logger.info(f"🗑️ Compte supprimé: {username_to_delete}")
    
    return {"detail": "Utilisateur supprimé"}

@app.post("/force_logout/{username}")
async def force_logout_user(username: str):
    if username in users:
        users[username]["online"] = False
        users[username]["last_logout"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
        save_data(users, messages)
        manager.disconnect(username)
        await manager.broadcast_status()
    
    return {"detail": "Utilisateur déconnecté"}

@app.post("/update_user_color")
async def update_user_color(data: dict):
    username = data.get("username", "").strip()
    color = data.get("color", "").strip()
    admin_username = data.get("admin_username", "").strip()
    
    if admin_username not in users or users[admin_username].get("role") != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Accès refusé")
    
    if username not in users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Utilisateur introuvable")
    
    if not color.startswith("#") or len(color) != 7:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Couleur invalide (format: #RRGGBB)")
    
    manager.set_user_color(username, color)
    
    # Notifier tous les clients du changement de couleur
    await manager.broadcast({
        "type": "user_color_updated",
        "username": username,
        "color": color
    })
    
    return {"detail": "Couleur mise à jour", "username": username, "color": color}

# Messages
@app.post("/send_message")
async def send_message(data: dict):
    sender = data.get("sender", "").strip()
    content = data.get("content", "").strip()
    file_url = data.get("file_url")
    file_name = data.get("file_name")
    file_size = data.get("file_size")

    if sender not in users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Utilisateur non autorisé")
    
    if not content and not file_url:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Message vide")
    
    # Vérifier la longueur du message
    if len(content) > CONFIG["max_message_length"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, 
                          detail=f"Message trop long (max {CONFIG['max_message_length']} caractères)")
    
    message_id = messages["last_id"]
    user_color = manager.get_user_color(sender)
    
    msg = {
        "id": message_id,
        "sender": sender,
        "content": content,
        "time": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "hour": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M"),
        "date": datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
        "file_url": file_url,
        "file_name": file_name,
        "file_size": file_size,
        "sender_color": user_color
    }
    
    messages["messages"].append(msg)
    messages["last_id"] += 1
    save_data(users, messages)
    
    logger.info(f"💬 {sender}: {content[:50] if content else 'Fichier'}")
    
    await manager.broadcast({
        "type": "new_message",
        "message": msg
    })
    
    return {"detail": "Message envoyé", "message_id": message_id}

@app.get("/messages")
async def get_messages(limit: int = 100, offset: int = 0):
    all_messages = messages["messages"]
    
    # Ajouter les couleurs aux messages existants
    for msg in all_messages:
        if "sender_color" not in msg:
            msg["sender_color"] = manager.get_user_color(msg["sender"])
    
    start = max(0, len(all_messages) - offset - limit)
    end = len(all_messages) - offset
    
    return {
        "messages": all_messages[start:end],
        "total": len(all_messages),
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset
    }

@app.post("/delete_message")
async def delete_message(data: dict):
    try:
        message_id = data.get("message_id")
        username = data.get("username")
        
        if not username or username not in users:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Non autorisé")
        
        # Chercher le message par son ID
        message_index = -1
        message_to_delete = None
        
        for i, msg in enumerate(messages["messages"]):
            if msg["id"] == message_id:
                message_index = i
                message_to_delete = msg
                break
        
        if message_index == -1 or not message_to_delete:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Message non trouvé")
        
        # Vérifier les permissions
        is_admin = users[username].get("role") == "admin"
        if message_to_delete["sender"] != username and not is_admin:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Vous ne pouvez supprimer que vos messages")
        
        # Supprimer le fichier physique si nécessaire
        if message_to_delete.get("file_url"):
            try:
                file_path = os.path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, os.path.basename(message_to_delete["file_url"]))
                if os.path.exists(file_path):
                    os.remove(file_path)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Erreur suppression fichier: {e}")
        
        # Supprimer le message du tableau
        del messages["messages"][message_index]
        
        # Réorganiser les IDs si nécessaire
        for i, msg in enumerate(messages["messages"]):
            msg["id"] = i
        messages["last_id"] = len(messages["messages"])
        
        save_data(users, messages)
        
        await manager.broadcast({
            "type": "delete_message",
            "message_id": message_id
        })
        
        logger.info(f"🗑️ Message {message_id} supprimé par {username}")
        
        return {"detail": "Message supprimé"}
        
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erreur suppression message: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Erreur interne du serveur")

# Upload de fichiers - PAS DE LIMITE DE TAILLE
@app.post("/upload_file")
async def upload_file(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    if not file:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Aucun fichier")
    
    try:
        # Liste étendue des extensions autorisées
        ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {
            '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp', '.bmp',
            '.pdf', '.txt', '.doc', '.docx', '.xls', '.xlsx', '.ppt', '.pptx',
            '.zip', '.rar', '.7z', '.tar', '.gz',
            '.mp3', '.m4a', '.wav', '.ogg', '.flac', '.aac',
            '.mp4', '.avi', '.mkv', '.mov', '.wmv', '.flv', '.webm',
            '.csv', '.json', '.xml', '.html', '.css', '.js', '.py', '.java', '.cpp', '.c',
            '.rtf', '.odt', '.ods', '.odp'
        }
        
        file_ext = os.path.splitext(file.filename)[1].lower()
        
        # Si config permet tous les types ou si extension est dans la liste
        if not CONFIG.get("allow_all_file_types", True) and file_ext not in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS:
            logger.warning(f"Tentative d'upload avec extension non autorisée: {file_ext}")
            # Accepter quand même avec avertissement
            # raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Type de fichier non autorisé")
        
        # Lire le fichier en chunks
        CHUNK_SIZE = 1024 * 1024  # 1MB chunks
        file_content = b""
        total_size = 0
        
        while True:
            chunk = await file.read(CHUNK_SIZE)
            if not chunk:
                break
            file_content += chunk
            total_size += len(chunk)
            
            # Log toutes les 10MB
            if total_size % (10 * 1024 * 1024) == 0:
                logger.info(f"Upload en cours: {file.filename} - {total_size / (1024*1024):.1f} MB")
        
        file_size = total_size
        
        # PAS DE VÉRIFICATION DE TAILLE - ACCEPTE TOUS LES FICHIERS
        logger.info(f"✅ Fichier uploadé: {file.filename} - Taille: {file_size} bytes ({file_size/(1024*1024):.1f} MB)")
        
        unique_filename = f"{uuid.uuid4()}{file_ext}"
        file_path = os.path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, unique_filename)
        
        # Écrire le fichier
        with open(file_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(file_content)
        
        file_url = f"/uploads/{unique_filename}"
        
        # Formater la taille
        def format_size(size):
            if size < 1024:
                return f"{size} B"
            elif size < 1024 * 1024:
                return f"{size/1024:.1f} KB"
            elif size < 1024 * 1024 * 1024:
                return f"{size/(1024*1024):.1f} MB"
            else:
                return f"{size/(1024*1024*1024):.1f} GB"
        
        return {
            "file_url": file_url,
            "file_name": file.filename,
            "file_size": format_size(file_size),
            "file_size_bytes": file_size,
            "detail": "Fichier uploadé avec succès"
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ Erreur upload fichier: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Erreur lors de l'upload: {str(e)}")

# Statistiques
@app.get("/stats")
async def get_stats():
    total_files = len([msg for msg in messages["messages"] if msg.get("file_url")])
    online_users = len(manager.active_connections)
    
    return {
        "total_users": len(users),
        "online_users": online_users,
        "total_messages": len(messages["messages"]),
        "total_files": total_files
    }

@app.post("/clear_messages")
async def clear_messages(data: dict):
    try:
        username = data.get("username", "")
        
        if username not in users or users[username].get("role") != "admin":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Accès refusé")
        
        # Sauvegarder l'ancien contenu pour le log
        old_message_count = len(messages["messages"])
        
        # Réinitialiser les messages
        messages["messages"] = []
        messages["last_id"] = 0
        save_data(users, messages)
        
        # Nettoyer les fichiers uploadés
        files_deleted = 0
        for filename in os.listdir(UPLOADS_DIR):
            file_path = os.path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, filename)
            try:
                if os.path.isfile(file_path):
                    os.remove(file_path)
                    files_deleted += 1
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Erreur suppression fichier {filename}: {e}")
        
        await manager.broadcast({
            "type": "clear_messages",
            "detail": "Historique supprimé"
        })
        
        logger.info(f"🧹 Historique vidé par {username}: {old_message_count} messages, {files_deleted} fichiers supprimés")
        
        return {"detail": "Historique supprimé", "messages_deleted": old_message_count, "files_deleted": files_deleted}
        
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erreur vidage historique: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Erreur interne du serveur")

# Sauvegarde
@app.post("/backup")
async def create_backup(data: dict):
    username = data.get("username", "")
    
    if username not in users or users[username].get("role") != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Accès refusé")
    
    backup_data = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "users": users,
        "messages": messages,
        "config": CONFIG
    }
    
    backup_file = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, f"backup_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.json")
    
    with open(backup_file, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(backup_data, f, indent=2)
    
    return {"detail": "Sauvegarde créée", "file": backup_file}

<script>
    // Configuration
    const CONFIG = {
        SERVER_IP: localStorage.getItem('chat_server_ip') || '192.168.129.5',
        SERVER_PORT: localStorage.getItem('chat_server_port') || '8000',
        AUTO_RECONNECT: true,
        MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS: 5,
        RECONNECT_DELAY: 3000,
        MESSAGE_REFRESH_INTERVAL: 5000,
        TYPING_TIMEOUT: 3000,
        // PAS DE LIMITE DE TAILLE
        MAX_FILE_SIZE: 10 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024, // 10GB (très grand)
        ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES: ['image/jpeg', 'image/jpg', 'image/png', 'image/gif', 'image/webp', 'image/bmp', 'image/svg+xml'],
        LONG_PRESS_DURATION: 1500, // 1.5 secondes pour l'appui long
        AUTO_SCROLL_ENABLED: true,
        MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH: 100000000000000000000 // Longueur max très augmentée
    };

    // État de l'application
    const STATE = {
        currentUser: null,
        serverUrl: null,
        websocket: null,
        isConnected: false,
        reconnectAttempts: 0,
        isTyping: false,
        typingTimeout: null,
        lastMessageId: null,
        onlineUsers: new Set(),
        userColors: new Map(),
        typingUsers: new Set(),
        messages: [],
        isLoadingMessages: false,
        selectedFiles: [],
        isDragging: false,
        shouldScrollToBottom: true,
        userColor: null,
        // Nouveau: gestion du menu contextuel
        contextMenu: {
            active: false,
            selectedMessage: null,
            selectedMessageId: null,
            touchTimer: null,
            isTouchDevice: 'ontouchstart' in window || navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0
        },
        uploadProgress: {
            currentFile: null,
            currentFileSize: 0,
            uploadedBytes: 0,
            xhr: null
        }
    };

    // Éléments DOM
    const DOM = {
        // Écrans
        loginScreen: document.getElementById('loginScreen'),
        chatScreen: document.getElementById('chatScreen'),
        
        // Header
        connectionStatus: document.getElementById('connectionStatus'),
        logoutBtn: document.getElementById('logoutBtn'),
        serverSettingsBtn: document.getElementById('serverSettingsBtn'),
        
        // Login
        serverIP: document.getElementById('serverIP'),
        serverPort: document.getElementById('serverPort'),
        username: document.getElementById('username'),
        password: document.getElementById('password'),
        loginBtn: document.getElementById('loginBtn'),
        loginNotification: document.getElementById('loginNotification'),
        serverSettings: document.getElementById('serverSettings'),
        cancelSettingsBtn: document.getElementById('cancelSettingsBtn'),
        saveSettingsBtn: document.getElementById('saveSettingsBtn'),
        
        // Chat
        currentUserName: document.getElementById('currentUserName'),
        userAvatar: document.getElementById('userAvatar'),
        onlineCount: document.getElementById('onlineCount'),
        onlineUsersBtn: document.getElementById('onlineUsersBtn'),
        onlineUsersPanel: document.getElementById('onlineUsersPanel'),
        usersList: document.getElementById('usersList'),
        typingIndicator: document.getElementById('typingIndicator'),
        typingUser: document.getElementById('typingUser'),
        messagesContainer: document.getElementById('messagesContainer'),
        messagesLoading: document.getElementById('messagesLoading'),
        messageInput: document.getElementById('messageInput'),
        sendBtn: document.getElementById('sendBtn'),
        attachFileBtn: document.getElementById('attachFileBtn'),
        
        // Modals
        fileModal: document.getElementById('fileModal'),
        fileDropArea: document.getElementById('fileDropArea'),
        fileInput: document.getElementById('fileInput'),
        selectedFile: document.getElementById('selectedFile'),
        fileMessage: document.getElementById('fileMessage'),
        closeFileModalBtn: document.getElementById('closeFileModalBtn'),
        cancelFileBtn: document.getElementById('cancelFileBtn'),
        uploadFileBtn: document.getElementById('uploadFileBtn'),
        imageViewerModal: document.getElementById('imageViewerModal'),
        viewerImage: document.getElementById('viewerImage'),
        imageInfo: document.getElementById('imageInfo'),
        
        // Upload progress
        uploadProgress: document.getElementById('uploadProgress'),
        progressFileName: document.getElementById('progressFileName'),
        progressPercent: document.getElementById('progressPercent'),
        progressFill: document.getElementById('progressFill'),
        
        // Drag & drop
        dragOverlay: document.getElementById('dragOverlay'),
        
        // Multiple files
        multipleFilesInfo: document.getElementById('multipleFilesInfo'),
        multipleFilesList: document.getElementById('multipleFilesList'),
        
        // Toast
        toastContainer: document.getElementById('toastContainer'),
        
        // Context Menu
        contextMenu: document.getElementById('contextMenu'),
        contextCopy: document.getElementById('contextCopy'),
        contextDownload: document.getElementById('contextDownload'),
        contextDelete: document.getElementById('contextDelete')
    };

    // Initialisation
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);

    async function init() {
        // Récupérer la configuration
        DOM.serverIP.value = CONFIG.SERVER_IP;
        DOM.serverPort.value = CONFIG.SERVER_PORT;
        
        // Vérifier la session existante
        await checkExistingSession();
        
        // Configurer les événements
        setupEventListeners();
        
        // Configurer le textarea auto-resize
        setupTextareaAutoResize();
        
        // Configurer le drag & drop global
        setupGlobalDragAndDrop();
        
        // Configurer le collage d'images
        setupPasteHandler();
        
        // Configurer le menu contextuel
        setupContextMenu();
        
        // Tester la connexion au serveur
        testServerConnection();
    }

    // Configurer le menu contextuel
    function setupContextMenu() {
        // Fermer le menu contextuel en cliquant ailleurs
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (!DOM.contextMenu.contains(e.target)) {
                hideContextMenu();
            }
        });
        
        // Gérer le clic droit (desktop)
        document.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
            if (STATE.currentUser) {
                const messageElement = e.target.closest('.message');
                if (messageElement && messageElement.dataset.messageId) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    showContextMenu(e.pageX, e.pageY, messageElement);
                }
            }
        });
        
        // Gérer le clic (pour fermer le menu)
        document.addEventListener('click', hideContextMenu);
        
        // Actions du menu contextuel
        DOM.contextCopy.addEventListener('click', handleCopyMessage);
        DOM.contextDownload.addEventListener('click', handleDownloadFromContext);
        DOM.contextDelete.addEventListener('click', handleDeleteMessageFromContext);
        
        // Configurer l'appui long pour mobile
        setupLongPressForMobile();
    }

    // Configurer l'appui long pour mobile
    function setupLongPressForMobile() {
        if (!STATE.contextMenu.isTouchDevice) return;
        
        let pressTimer;
        
        document.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
            if (!STATE.currentUser) return;
            
            const messageElement = e.target.closest('.message');
            if (messageElement && messageElement.dataset.messageId) {
                // Empêcher le menu contextuel par défaut
                e.preventDefault();
                
                // Démarrer le timer pour l'appui long
                pressTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    // Calculer la position pour le menu
                    const touch = e.touches[0] || e.changedTouches[0];
                    showContextMenu(touch.pageX, touch.pageY, messageElement);
                }, CONFIG.LONG_PRESS_DURATION);
                
                // Ajouter l'effet visuel
                messageElement.classList.add('highlight');
            }
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
            clearTimeout(pressTimer);
            const messageElement = e.target.closest('.message');
            if (messageElement) {
                messageElement.classList.remove('highlight');
            }
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
            clearTimeout(pressTimer);
            const messageElement = e.target.closest('.message');
            if (messageElement) {
                messageElement.classList.remove('highlight');
            }
        });
    }

    // Afficher le menu contextuel
    function showContextMenu(x, y, messageElement) {
        STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessage = messageElement;
        STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessageId = messageElement.dataset.messageId;
        
        // Vérifier si le message a des fichiers
        const hasFile = messageElement.querySelector('.file-attachment, .image-preview img, .audio-player audio');
        DOM.contextDownload.style.display = hasFile ? 'flex' : 'none';
        
        DOM.contextMenu.style.display = 'block';
        
        // Positionner le menu
        const menuWidth = DOM.contextMenu.offsetWidth;
        const menuHeight = DOM.contextMenu.offsetHeight;
        const windowWidth = window.innerWidth;
        const windowHeight = window.innerHeight;
        
        // Ajuster la position si le menu dépasse de l'écran
        let posX = x;
        let posY = y;
        
        if (x + menuWidth > windowWidth) {
            posX = windowWidth - menuWidth - 10;
        }
        
        if (y + menuHeight > windowHeight) {
            posY = windowHeight - menuHeight - 10;
        }
        
        DOM.contextMenu.style.left = posX + 'px';
        DOM.contextMenu.style.top = posY + 'px';
        STATE.contextMenu.active = true;
        
        // Sur mobile, ajouter un effet visuel
        if (STATE.contextMenu.isTouchDevice) {
            messageElement.classList.add('highlight');
            setTimeout(() => {
                messageElement.classList.remove('highlight');
            }, 500);
        }
    }

    // Cacher le menu contextuel
    function hideContextMenu() {
        DOM.contextMenu.style.display = 'none';
        STATE.contextMenu.active = false;
        STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessage = null;
        STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessageId = null;
    }

    // Copier le message
    function handleCopyMessage() {
        if (!STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessage) return;
        
        const messageElement = STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessage;
        const contentElement = messageElement.querySelector('.message-content');
        const fileElement = messageElement.querySelector('.file-name');
        
        let textToCopy = '';
        
        if (contentElement) {
            textToCopy += contentElement.textContent + '\n';
        }
        
        if (fileElement) {
            textToCopy += `Fichier: ${fileElement.textContent}\n`;
        }
        
        // Copier dans le presse-papier
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy.trim())
            .then(() => {
                showToast('Message copié dans le presse-papier', 'success');
            })
            .catch(err => {
                console.error('Erreur de copie:', err);
                showToast('Erreur lors de la copie', 'error');
            });
        
        hideContextMenu();
    }

    // Télécharger depuis le menu contextuel
    function handleDownloadFromContext() {
        if (!STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessage) return;
        
        const messageElement = STATE.contextMenu.selectedMessage;
        
        // Chercher différents types de fichiers
        const fileAttachment = messageElement.querySelector('.file-attachment');
        const imagePreview = messageElement.querySelector('.image-preview img');
        const audioPlayer = messageElement.querySelector('.audio-player audio source');
        
        if (fileAttachment && fileAttachment.href) {
            // Télécharger le fichier attaché
            const url = fileAttachment.href;
            const fileName = fileAttachment.querySelector('.file-name')?.textContent || `file_${Date.now()}`;
            forceDownload(url, fileName);
        } else if (imagePreview && imagePreview.src) {
            // Télécharger l'image
            const url = imagePreview.src;
