<script>
// ============================================
// VARIABLES GLOBALES
// ============================================
const APP_STORAGE_KEY = 'global_calculator_data_v9';
const BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY = 'calculator_backups';
const SETTINGS_KEY = 'calculator_settings_v5';
const RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY = 'recent_calculations';
const FUSION_STATES_KEY = 'fusion_states';
const SHORTCUTS_KEY = 'keyboard_shortcuts_v1';
const NOTES_STORAGE_KEY = 'multiline_notes';
const EXCHANGE_RATES_KEY = 'exchange_rates_cache';

// Clé API gratuite pour les taux de change (vous pouvez créer un compte gratuit sur exchangerate-api.com)
const EXCHANGE_API_KEY = 'd7a4c2123055ac48199a67cc'; // Clé de test publique
const EXCHANGE_API_URL = 'https://v6.exchangerate-api.com/v6';

let chartD = null, chartF = null;
let dDates = [], dSeries = [];
let historiqueF = [];
let fusionStates = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(FUSION_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
let conversionHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('conversionHistory') || '[]');
let recentCalculations = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY) || '[]');
let cBlocks = [];
let cBlockCounter = 1;
let exchangeRates = {};
let currentTheme = 'light';
let calculatorHistory = [];
let calcExpression = '';
let calcResult = '0';
let settings = {};
let keyboardShortcuts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SHORTCUTS_KEY) || '[]');

// Variables pour le nouveau mode Budget
let budgetCategories = [];
let budgetCatCount = 1;

// Variables pour le widget de notes
let notes = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NOTES_STORAGE_KEY)) || [];
let currentTab = 'audio';
let editingNoteId = null;
let recognition;
let isDictating = false;
let finalTranscriptAccumulated = "";
let mediaRecorder;
let chunks = [];
let isRecording = false;
let audioCtx = null;
let gainNodes = {};
let audioElements = {};

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DU WIDGET NOTES - CORRIGÉES
// ============================================
function toggleNotesPanel() {
    document.getElementById('notesPanel').classList.toggle('active');
    cancelEdit();
    renderNotes();
}

function switchTab(tab) {
    currentTab = tab;
    document.getElementById('tabAudio').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'audio');
    document.getElementById('tabText').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'text');
    cancelEdit();
    renderNotes();
}

function renderNotes() {
    const container = document.getElementById('notesList');
    const filtered = notes.filter(n => n.type === currentTab);
    
    if(filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; margin-top:50px; opacity:0.3;">Aucune note ici</div>';
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = filtered.map(n => {
        let contentHTML = "";
        if (n.subType === 'checklist') {
            contentHTML = `<div class="checklist-container">
                ${n.items.map((item, idx) => `
                    <div class="check-item ${item.checked ? 'done' : ''}">
                        <input type="checkbox" ${item.checked ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleCheckItem(${n.id}, ${idx})">
                        <span onclick="editCheckItem(${n.id}, ${idx})">${item.text}</span>
                        <i class="fas fa-times" onclick="removeCheckItem(${n.id}, ${idx})" style="margin-left:auto; opacity:0.3; cursor:pointer;"></i>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
                <input type="text" class="add-check-input" placeholder="+ Ajouter..." onkeypress="handleCheckEntry(event, ${n.id})">
            </div>`;
        } else if (n.type === 'text') {
            contentHTML = `<div class="text-content" onclick="openEditor(${n.id})">${n.content || "Note vide..."}</div>`;
        } else {
            // CORRECTION: Audio avec boost fonctionnel
            contentHTML = `<audio id="audio-${n.id}" src="${n.content}" controls style="width:100%; margin-bottom: 5px;"></audio>
                           <div style="font-size:0.8em; margin-top:5px; opacity:0.7; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
                           <i class="fas fa-volume-up"></i> Boost: 
                           <select id="boost-select-${n.id}" onchange="boostAudio(${n.id}, this.value)" style="background:var(--widget-bg); color:white; border:1px solid #444; padding: 3px 6px; border-radius: 4px;">
                           <option value="1">100%</option>
                           <option value="2">200%</option>
                           <option value="3">300%</option>
                           <option value="4">400%</option>
                           <option value="5">500%</option></select></div>`;
        }

        return `
            <div class="note-item">
                <div class="note-header">
                    <div class="note-title" onclick="renameNote(${n.id})">
                        <i class="fas fa-pen-nib" style="font-size:0.8em; opacity:0.6"></i> ${n.name || n.date}
                    </div>
                    <i class="fas fa-trash" onclick="deleteNote(${n.id})" style="color:var(--error-color); cursor:pointer;"></i>
                </div>
                ${contentHTML}
            </div>
        `;
    }).join('');
}

// --- ÉDITEUR MULTI-LIGNES ---
function openEditor(id = null) {
    editingNoteId = id;
    const editor = document.getElementById('textEditorUI');
    const list = document.getElementById('notesList');
    const titleInput = document.getElementById('editTitle');
    const textInput = document.getElementById('editText');

    if (id) {
        const note = notes.find(n => n.id === id);
        titleInput.value = note.name || "";
        textInput.value = note.content || "";
    } else {
        titleInput.value = "Nouvelle Note";
        textInput.value = "";
    }

    list.style.display = 'none';
    editor.style.display = 'flex';
    textInput.focus();
}

function cancelEdit() {
    document.getElementById('textEditorUI').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('notesList').style.display = 'block';
    editingNoteId = null;
}

function saveTextNote() {
    const title = document.getElementById('editTitle').value || "Sans titre";
    const content = document.getElementById('editText').value;

    if (editingNoteId) {
        const note = notes.find(n => n.id === editingNoteId);
        note.name = title;
        note.content = content;
    } else {
        notes.unshift({
            id: Date.now(),
            type: 'text',
            subType: 'free',
            name: title,
            content: content,
            date: new Date().toLocaleString()
        });
    }

    saveNotes();
    cancelEdit();
    switchTab('text');
}

// --- CHECKLIST ---
function createNewChecklist() {
    const name = prompt("Nom de la liste :");
    if (!name) return;
    notes.unshift({
        id: Date.now(),
        type: 'text',
        subType: 'checklist',
        name: name,
        date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
        items: []
    });
    saveNotes();
    switchTab('text');
}

function handleCheckEntry(e, nid) {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target.value.trim()) {
        notes.find(n => n.id === nid).items.push({ text: e.target.value, checked: false });
        e.target.value = "";
        saveNotes();
    }
}

function toggleCheckItem(nid, idx) {
    notes.find(n => n.id === nid).items[idx].checked = !notes.find(n => n.id === nid).items[idx].checked;
    saveNotes();
}

function removeCheckItem(nid, idx) {
    notes.find(n => n.id === nid).items.splice(idx, 1);
    saveNotes();
}

function editCheckItem(nid, idx) {
    const newText = prompt("Modifier l'élément :", notes.find(n => n.id === nid).items[idx].text);
    if (newText !== null) {
        notes.find(n => n.id === nid).items[idx].text = newText;
        saveNotes();
    }
}

// --- FONCTIONS SYSTÈME NOTES ---
function saveNotes() {
    localStorage.setItem(NOTES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(notes));
    renderNotes();
}

function deleteNote(id) {
    if (confirm("Supprimer cette note ?")) {
        notes = notes.filter(n => n.id !== id);
        saveNotes();
    }
}

function renameNote(id) {
    const n = notes.find(n => n.id === id);
    const nn = prompt("Renommer :", n.name || n.date);
    if (nn) {
        n.name = nn;
        saveNotes();
    }
}

// --- DICTÉE & AUDIO - CORRIGÉ ---
function initAudioContext() {
    if (!audioCtx) {
        audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        gainNodes = {};
        
        // Gérer la suspension automatique du navigateur
        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
            const resumeAudio = async () => {
                await audioCtx.resume();
                console.log('AudioContext résumé');
            };
            resumeAudio();
        }
        
        // Détecter les clics pour réactiver l'audio
        document.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
                await audioCtx.resume();
            }
        });
    }
    return audioCtx;
}

function boostAudio(id, multiplier) {
    try {
        const audioElement = document.getElementById(`audio-${id}`);
        if (!audioElement) return;
        
        // Initialiser le contexte audio
        const ctx = initAudioContext();
        
        // Si nous n'avons pas encore créé de nœud de gain pour cet audio
        if (!gainNodes[id]) {
            const source = ctx.createMediaElementSource(audioElement);
            const gainNode = ctx.createGain();
            
            // Connecter la source au gainNode et le gainNode à la destination
            source.connect(gainNode);
            gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);
            
            // Sauvegarder le nœud de gain
            gainNodes[id] = gainNode;
        }
        
        // Appliquer le multiplicateur de volume
        const gainValue = parseFloat(multiplier);
        gainNodes[id].gain.value = gainValue;
        
        // Mettre à jour l'affichage du volume
        audioElement.volume = Math.min(gainValue, 5) / 5;
        
        console.log(`Boost audio ${id}: x${gainValue}`);
        showNotification(`Volume boosté x${gainValue}`, 'info', 1000);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erreur boost audio:', error);
        // Fallback: ajuster le volume directement
        const audioElement = document.getElementById(`audio-${id}`);
        if (audioElement) {
            audioElement.volume = Math.min(parseFloat(multiplier), 5) / 5;
        }
    }
}

// Initialisation de la reconnaissance vocale améliorée
function initSpeechRecognition() {
    if ('webkitSpeechRecognition' in window || 'SpeechRecognition' in window) {
        const SpeechRecognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
        recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
        recognition.lang = 'fr-FR';
        recognition.continuous = true;
        recognition.interimResults = true;
        recognition.maxAlternatives = 1;
        
        recognition.onresult = (e) => {
            let interim = '';
            for (let i = e.resultIndex; i < e.results.length; ++i) {
                if (e.results[i].isFinal) {
                    const transcript = e.results[i][0].transcript;
                    finalTranscriptAccumulated += transcript + " ";
                    
                    // Lecture fluide du texte reconnu
                    speakText(transcript);
                } else {
                    interim += e.results[i][0].transcript;
                }
            }
            document.getElementById('interimText').innerText = finalTranscriptAccumulated + interim;
        };
        
        recognition.onend = () => {
            if (isDictating) {
                setTimeout(() => recognition.start(), 100);
            }
        };
        
        recognition.onerror = (e) => {
            console.error('Erreur reconnaissance vocale:', e.error);
            if (e.error === 'not-allowed') {
                showNotification('Microphone non autorisé', 'error');
            }
        };
    }
}

// Lecture fluide des messages texte
function speakText(text) {
    if ('speechSynthesis' in window && text.trim()) {
        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
        utterance.lang = 'fr-FR';
        utterance.rate = 1.0;
        utterance.pitch = 1.0;
        utterance.volume = 1.0;
        
        // Utiliser une voix féminine française si disponible
        const voices = speechSynthesis.getVoices();
        const frenchVoice = voices.find(voice => voice.lang === 'fr-FR' && voice.name.includes('female'));
        if (frenchVoice) {
            utterance.voice = frenchVoice;
        }
        
        speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }
}

function toggleDictation() {
    if (!recognition) {
        initSpeechRecognition();
    }
    
    if (isDictating) {
        isDictating = false;
        recognition.stop();
        if (finalTranscriptAccumulated.trim()) {
            notes.unshift({
                id: Date.now(),
                type: 'text',
                subType: 'free',
                content: finalTranscriptAccumulated.trim(),
                date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
                name: "Dictée Vocale"
            });
            saveNotes();
            showNotification('Dictée enregistrée', 'success');
        }
        document.getElementById('dictationUI').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('btnDictate').classList.remove('btn-stop');
        document.getElementById('btnDictate').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-keyboard"></i> Dictée';
    } else {
        // Demander la permission d'abord
        navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true })
            .then(() => {
                isDictating = true;
                finalTranscriptAccumulated = "";
                recognition.start();
                document.getElementById('dictationUI').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('btnDictate').classList.add('btn-stop');
                document.getElementById('btnDictate').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone-slash"></i> Arrêter';
                switchTab('text');
                showNotification('Dictée démarrée - Parlez maintenant', 'info');
            })
            .catch(err => {
                showNotification('Permission microphone refusée', 'error');
                console.error('Microphone error:', err);
            });
    }
}

async function toggleAudio() {
    if (isRecording) {
        mediaRecorder.stop();
        isRecording = false;
        document.getElementById('btnRecord').classList.remove('btn-stop');
        document.getElementById('btnRecord').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-microphone"></i> Audio';
        showNotification('Enregistrement arrêté', 'info');
    } else {
        try {
            // Demander la permission d'abord
            const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ 
                audio: {
                    echoCancellation: true,
                    noiseSuppression: true,
                    autoGainControl: true,
                    channelCount: 1,
                    sampleRate: 44100
                } 
            });
            
            // Initialiser le contexte audio pour les boosts
            initAudioContext();
            
            mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, {
                mimeType: 'audio/webm;codecs=opus'
            });
            chunks = [];
            
            mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = e => {
                if (e.data.size > 0) chunks.push(e.data);
            };
            
            mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
                const b = new Blob(chunks, { type: 'audio/webm' });
                const r = new FileReader();
                r.readAsDataURL(b);
                r.onloadend = () => {
                    const newNote = {
                        id: Date.now(),
                        type: 'audio',
                        content: r.result,
                        date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
                        name: "Enregistrement " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})
                    };
                    notes.unshift(newNote);
                    saveNotes();
                    
                    // Attendre un peu pour que l'audio soit rendu, puis initialiser le boost
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        boostAudio(newNote.id, '1');
                        // Lire automatiquement l'enregistrement
                        const audioElement = document.getElementById(`audio-${newNote.id}`);
                        if (audioElement) {
                            audioElement.play().catch(e => console.log('Lecture auto ignorée:', e));
                        }
                    }, 500);
                };
                
                // Arrêter toutes les pistes du stream
                stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            };
            
            mediaRecorder.onerror = (e) => {
                console.error('Erreur enregistrement:', e);
                showNotification('Erreur d\'enregistrement', 'error');
            };
            
            mediaRecorder.start();
            isRecording = true;
            document.getElementById('btnRecord').classList.add('btn-stop');
            document.getElementById('btnRecord').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-stop"></i> Arrêter';
            switchTab('audio');
            showNotification('Enregistrement démarré', 'success');
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Erreur accès microphone:', error);
            if (error.name === 'NotAllowedError') {
                showNotification('Permission microphone refusée', 'error');
            } else if (error.name === 'NotFoundError') {
                showNotification('Microphone non trouvé', 'error');
            } else {
                showNotification('Erreur d\'accès au microphone', 'error');
            }
        }
    }
}

});

// ============================================
// TABLEAU DE BORD - ACCUEIL UTILE
// ============================================
function updateDashboard() {
    updateRecentCalculations();
    updateDashboardStats();
}

function updateRecentCalculations() {
    const container = document.getElementById('recentCalculations');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const allRecent = [
        ...recentCalculations,
        ...calculatorHistory.slice(0, 3).map(item => ({
            type: 'calculatrice',
            title: `Calculatrice: ${item.expression}`,
            value: item.result,
            timestamp: item.timestamp,
            action: () => showMode('modeCalculator')
        })),
        ...conversionHistory.slice(0, 2).map(item => ({
            type: 'devise',
            title: `Conversion: ${item.from.amount} ${item.from.currency} → ${item.to.currency}`,
            value: item.to.amount,
            timestamp: item.date,
            action: () => showMode('modeH')
        }))
    ].sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp || b.date) - new Date(a.timestamp || a.date)).slice(0, 5);
    
    if (allRecent.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-history fa-2x" style="margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.5;"></i>
                <p>Aucun calcul récent</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    allRecent.forEach(item => {
        const date = item.timestamp || item.date || 'Récemment';
        const value = typeof item.value === 'number' ? item.value.toFixed(2) : item.value;
        
        html += `
            <div class="recent-item" onclick="${item.action ? item.action.toString().replace('function', '').replace('()', '') : 'showMode(\'modeCalculator\')'}">
                <div>
                    <div style="font-weight: bold;">${item.title}</div>
                    <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${date}</div>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <span class="recent-type">${item.type}</span>
                    <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--accent-color); text-align: right; margin-top: 5px;">${value}</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function updateDashboardStats() {
    const today = new Date().toDateString();
    const todayCalculations = calculatorHistory.filter(item => 
        new Date(item.timestamp).toDateString() === today
    ).length;
    
    document.getElementById('statCalculs').textContent = todayCalculations;
    document.getElementById('statConversions').textContent = conversionHistory.length;
    document.getElementById('statGraphs').textContent = (chartD ? 1 : 0) + (chartF ? 1 : 0);
    document.getElementById('statBlocs').textContent = cBlocks.length + budgetCategories.length + notes.length;
}

function addRecentCalculation(type, title, value) {
    const calculation = {
        type: type,
        title: title,
        value: value,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR')
    };
    
    recentCalculations.unshift(calculation);
    if (recentCalculations.length > 20) {
        recentCalculations = recentCalculations.slice(0, 20);
    }
    
    localStorage.setItem(RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(recentCalculations));
    updateRecentCalculations();
}

function clearRecentCalculations() {
    if (recentCalculations.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirm('Effacer tous les calculs récents ?')) {
        recentCalculations = [];
        localStorage.setItem(RECENT_CALCULATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify([]));
        updateRecentCalculations();
        showNotification('Calculs récents effacés', 'success');
    }
}

function quickCalculation(type) {
    switch(type) {
        case 'percentage':
            const value = prompt('Entrez la valeur :', '100');
            const percentage = prompt('Entrez le pourcentage :', '20');
            if (value && percentage) {
                const result = (parseFloat(value) * parseFloat(percentage)) / 100;
                showMode('modeCalculator');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    addToExpression(`${value}*${percentage}/100`);
                    calculateResult();
                    addRecentCalculation('pourcentage', 
                        `Calcul de ${percentage}% de ${value}`, 
                        result
                    );
                }, 100);
            }
            break;
            
        case 'vat':
            const ht = prompt('Montant HT :', '100');
            const tva = prompt('Taux TVA (%) :', '20');
            if (ht && tva) {
                const ttc = parseFloat(ht) * (1 + parseFloat(tva)/100);
                showMode('modeG');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    addRecentCalculation('tva', 
                        `TVA: ${ht} HT → ${ttc.toFixed(2)} TTC (${tva}%)`, 
                        ttc
                    );
                }, 100);
            }
            break;
            
        case 'currency':
            const amount = prompt('Montant à convertir :', '100');
            if (amount) {
                showMode('modeH');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    document.getElementById('fromAmount').value = amount;
                    convertCurrency();
                    addRecentCalculation('devise', 
                        `Conversion: ${amount} EUR → USD`, 
                        parseFloat(document.getElementById('toAmount').value || 0)
                    );
                }, 100);
            }
            break;
    }
}

// ============================================
// GESTION DU THÈME

function toggleTheme() {
    currentTheme = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light';
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', currentTheme);
    localStorage.setItem('calculator_theme', currentTheme);
    saveSettings();
    
    const themeIcon = document.querySelector('.theme-toggle i');
    themeIcon.className = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
    
    showNotification(`Thème ${currentTheme === 'light' ? 'clair' : 'sombre'} activé`, 'info');
}

function loadTheme() {
    const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('calculator_theme') || 'light';
    currentTheme = savedTheme;
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', currentTheme);
    
    const themeIcon = document.querySelector('.theme-toggle i');
    if (themeIcon) {
        themeIcon.className = currentTheme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
    }
}

// ============================================
// RACCOURCIS CLAVIER
// ============================================
function initKeyboardShortcuts() {
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(event) {
        const key = getKeyCombination(event);
        
        // Vérifier si un raccourci correspond
        const shortcut = keyboardShortcuts.find(s => s.key === key);
        if (shortcut) {
            event.preventDefault();
            showMode(shortcut.mode);
            showNotification(`Raccourci activé : ${shortcut.name}`, 'info');
        }
    });
    
    // Mettre à jour l'affichage des raccourcis
    updateShortcutsDisplay();
}

function getKeyCombination(event) {
    const keys = [];
    if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) keys.push('Ctrl');
    if (event.altKey) keys.push('Alt');
    if (event.shiftKey) keys.push('Shift');
    
    // Ignorer les touches de modification seules
    if (!['Control', 'Alt', 'Shift', 'Meta'].includes(event.key)) {
        const key = event.key.length === 1 ? event.key.toUpperCase() : event.key;
        keys.push(key);
    }
    
    return keys.join('+');
}

function addShortcut() {
    const keyInput = document.getElementById('shortcutKey');
    const modeSelect = document.getElementById('shortcutMode');
    
    const key = keyInput.value.trim();
    const mode = modeSelect.value;
    
    if (!key) {
        showNotification('Veuillez entrer un raccourci clavier', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    // Vérifier si le raccourci existe déjà
    if (keyboardShortcuts.some(s => s.key === key)) {
        showNotification('Ce raccourci existe déjà', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    // Nom automatique basé sur le mode
    const modeNames = {
        'home': 'Accueil',
        'modeCalculator': 'Calculatrice',
        'modeA': 'Comparer',
        'modeC': '% Bloc',
        'modeB': 'Budget',
        'modeD': 'Évolution',
        'modeF': 'Circulaire',
        'modeG': 'TVA',
        'modeH': 'Devises'
    };
    
    const shortcut = {
        id: Date.now(),
        key: key,
        mode: mode,
        name: `${modeNames[mode]} (${key})`
    };
    
    keyboardShortcuts.push(shortcut);
    updateShortcutsDisplay();
    keyInput.value = '';
    
    showNotification('Raccourci ajouté', 'success');
}

function updateShortcutsDisplay() {
    const container = document.getElementById('shortcutsList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (keyboardShortcuts.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-keyboard"></i>
                <p>Aucun raccourci défini</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    keyboardShortcuts.forEach(shortcut => {
        html += `
            <div class="shortcut-item">
                <div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 5px;">${shortcut.name}</div>
                    <div class="shortcut-meta">
                        <span class="shortcut-key">${shortcut.key}</span>
                        → ${shortcut.mode}
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="shortcut-actions">
                    <button onclick="deleteShortcut(${shortcut.id})" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 5px 10px;">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function deleteShortcut(id) {
    keyboardShortcuts = keyboardShortcuts.filter(s => s.id !== id);
    updateShortcutsDisplay();
    showNotification('Raccourci supprimé', 'success');
}

// ============================================
// PARAMÈTRES
// ============================================
function showSettings() {
    document.getElementById('settingsModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    updateShortcutsDisplay();
}

function closeSettings() {
    document.getElementById('settingsModal').classList.add('hidden');
}

function loadSettings() {
    const defaultSettings = {
        autoSave: true,
        notifications: true,
        decimalPlaces: 2,
        defaultCurrency: 'EUR',
        autoRound: true,
        defaultTheme: 'light'
    };
    
    const savedSettings = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY) || '{}');
    settings = { ...defaultSettings, ...savedSettings };
    
    // Appliquer les paramètres aux contrôles
    document.getElementById('autoSave').checked = settings.autoSave;
    document.getElementById('notifications').checked = settings.notifications;
    document.getElementById('decimalPlaces').value = settings.decimalPlaces;
    document.getElementById('defaultCurrency').value = settings.defaultCurrency;
    document.getElementById('autoRound').checked = settings.autoRound;
    document.getElementById('defaultTheme').value = settings.defaultTheme;
}

function saveSettings() {
    settings.autoSave = document.getElementById('autoSave').checked;
    settings.notifications = document.getElementById('notifications').checked;
    settings.decimalPlaces = parseInt(document.getElementById('decimalPlaces').value);
    settings.defaultCurrency = document.getElementById('defaultCurrency').value;
    settings.autoRound = document.getElementById('autoRound').checked;
    settings.defaultTheme = document.getElementById('defaultTheme').value;
    
    // Sauvegarder les raccourcis
    localStorage.setItem(SHORTCUTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(keyboardShortcuts));
    
    localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));
    showNotification('Paramètres enregistrés', 'success');
    closeSettings();
}

function resetSettings() {
    if (confirm('Réinitialiser tous les paramètres aux valeurs par défaut ?')) {
        localStorage.removeItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
        localStorage.removeItem(SHORTCUTS_KEY);
        settings = {};
        keyboardShortcuts = [];
        loadSettings();
        updateShortcutsDisplay();
        showNotification('Paramètres réinitialisés', 'success');
    }
}

function formatNumber(value) {
    const decimalPlaces = settings.decimalPlaces || 2;
    const rounded = settings.autoRound ? parseFloat(value).toFixed(decimalPlaces) : value;
    return parseFloat(rounded);
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS GÉNÉRALES
// ============================================
function showMode(modeId) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.mode-content').forEach(el => {
        el.classList.add('hidden');
    });
    
    if (modeId !== 'none') {
        document.getElementById(modeId).classList.remove('hidden');
        
        // Initialiser les modes si besoin
        if (modeId === 'modeC' && cBlocks.length === 0) addCBlock();
        if (modeId === 'modeH') loadExchangeRates();
        if (modeId === 'modeF') {
            autoCalculF();
            loadFusionStates();
        }
        if (modeId === 'modeD') updateDTable();
        if (modeId === 'home') updateDashboard();
        if (modeId === 'modeB' && budgetCategories.length === 0) {
            addBudgetCategory();
        }
        
        // Fermer le panneau de notes si ouvert
        document.getElementById('notesPanel').classList.remove('active');
        
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function removeNode(btn) {
    const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
    if (block) {
        block.remove();
        if (btn.closest('#multiFContainer')) autoCalculF();
        if (btn.closest('#multiGContainer')) autoCalculG();
        if (btn.closest('#multiCContainer')) updateCResults();
        if (btn.closest('#budgetCategories')) updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function parseNumber(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') value = value.replace(',', '.');
    const num = parseFloat(value);
    return isNaN(num) ? 0 : num;
}

function showNotification(message, type = 'info', duration = 3000) {
    if (settings.notifications === false) return;
    
    const container = document.getElementById('notificationContainer');
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = `notification ${type}`;
    
    const icons = {
        success: 'fas fa-check-circle',
        error: 'fas fa-exclamation-circle',
        warning: 'fas fa-exclamation-triangle',
        info: 'fas fa-info-circle'
    };
    
    notification.innerHTML = `
        <i class="${icons[type] || icons.info}"></i>
        <span>${message}</span>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(notification);
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (notification.parentNode) {
            notification.style.animation = 'slideIn 0.3s ease reverse';
            setTimeout(() => notification.remove(), 300);
        }
    }, duration);
}

function showNotificationAutoSave(message) {
    const container = document.getElementById('notificationContainer');
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = 'notification info';
    notification.style.background = 'var(--success-color)';
    notification.style.opacity = '0.9';
    notification.style.fontSize = '12px';
    notification.style.padding = '8px 12px';
    
    notification.innerHTML = `
        <i class="fas fa-save"></i>
        <span>${message}</span>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(notification);
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (notification.parentNode) {
            notification.style.animation = 'slideIn 0.3s ease reverse';
            setTimeout(() => notification.remove(), 300);
        }
    }, 1500);
}

// ============================================
// CALCULATRICE SCIENTIFIQUE
// ============================================
function addToExpression(value) {
    const expressionDiv = document.getElementById('calcExpression');
    const resultDiv = document.getElementById('calcResult');
    
    if (value === 'pi') {
        calcExpression += Math.PI;
    } else {
        calcExpression += value;
    }
    
    expressionDiv.textContent = calcExpression;
    resultDiv.textContent = calcExpression || '0';
}

function clearCalculator() {
    calcExpression = '';
    calcResult = '0';
    document.getElementById('calcExpression').textContent = '';
    document.getElementById('calcResult').textContent = '0';
}

function deleteLast() {
    calcExpression = calcExpression.slice(0, -1);
    document.getElementById('calcExpression').textContent = calcExpression;
    document.getElementById('calcResult').textContent = calcExpression || '0';
}

function calculateResult() {
    try {
        let expression = calcExpression;
        
        expression = expression.replace(/sqrt\(/g, 'Math.sqrt(');
        expression = expression.replace(/\^/g, '**');
        expression = expression.replace(/sin\(/g, 'Math.sin(');
        expression = expression.replace(/cos\(/g, 'Math.cos(');
        expression = expression.replace(/tan\(/g, 'Math.tan(');
        expression = expression.replace(/log\(/g, 'Math.log10(');
        expression = expression.replace(/ln\(/g, 'Math.log(');
        
        const result = eval(expression);
        
        if (isNaN(result) || !isFinite(result)) {
            throw new Error('Résultat non valide');
        }
        
        calcResult = result.toString();
        document.getElementById('calcResult').textContent = formatNumber(result);
        
        saveToCalcHistory(calcExpression, result);
        addRecentCalculation('calculatrice', `Calcul: ${calcExpression}`, result);
        
        calcExpression = '';
        document.getElementById('calcExpression').textContent = '';
        
        saveAllData();
        
    } catch (error) {
        showNotification('Erreur de calcul : ' + error.message, 'error');
        document.getElementById('calcResult').textContent = 'Erreur';
    }
}

function saveToCalcHistory(expression, result) {
    const historyEntry = {
        id: Date.now(),
        expression: expression,
        result: result,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR')
    };
    
    calculatorHistory.unshift(historyEntry);
    if (calculatorHistory.length > 20) {
        calculatorHistory = calculatorHistory.slice(0, 20);
    }
    
    updateCalcHistoryDisplay();
}

function updateCalcHistoryDisplay() {
    const container = document.getElementById('calcHistoryList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (calculatorHistory.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Aucun calcul récent</div>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    calculatorHistory.forEach(entry => {
        html += `
            <div style="padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-light);">
                <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${entry.timestamp}</div>
                <div style="font-weight: bold;">${entry.expression} = ${formatNumber(entry.result)}</div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function clearCalcHistory() {
    if (calculatorHistory.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirm('Effacer tout l\'historique des calculs ?')) {
        calculatorHistory = [];
        updateCalcHistoryDisplay();
        showNotification('Historique des calculs effacé', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

// ============================================
// FONCTIONS DES MODES EXISTANTS
// ============================================

// Mode A
function addABlock() {
    const cont = document.getElementById("multiAContainer");
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.innerHTML = `
        <input type="number" class="refA" placeholder="Référence" value="100" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculA()">
        <input type="text" class="valsA" placeholder="Valeurs (séparées par espaces)" value="80 120 100" style="flex:1;" oninput="autoCalculA()">
        <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
    `;
    cont.appendChild(div);
}

function autoCalculA() {
    const cont = document.getElementById("multiAContainer");
    const resDiv = document.getElementById("resultA");
    resDiv.innerHTML = "";
    
    Array.from(cont.getElementsByClassName("ref-block")).forEach((block, index) => {
        const ref = parseNumber(block.querySelector(".refA").value);
        const raw = (block.querySelector(".valsA").value || "").trim();
        if (raw === "") return;
        const vals = raw.split(/\s+/).map(parseNumber).filter(v => v !== null);
        
        let html = `<div style="margin:15px 0;"><h4>Bloc ${index + 1} - Référence : ${ref.toFixed(2)}€</h4>`;
        html += `<table style="width:100%;"><tr><th>Valeur</th><th>Différence</th><th>%</th></tr>`;
        
        vals.forEach(v => {
            const diff = v - ref;
            const pct = (ref === 0) ? null : (diff / ref * 100);
            const col = diff > 0 ? "var(--success-color)" : diff < 0 ? "var(--error-color)" : "var(--text-muted)";
            html += `<tr style="background:${col}; color:#fff;">
                <td>${v.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                <td>${diff>=0?'+':''}${diff.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                <td>${pct===null?'—':pct.toFixed(2)+'%'}</td>
            </tr>`;
        });
        html += "</table></div>";
        resDiv.innerHTML += html;
    });
    saveAllData();
}

function collectModeAData() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiAContainer .ref-block');
    return Array.from(blocks).map(block => ({
        ref: block.querySelector('.refA').value,
        vals: block.querySelector('.valsA').value
    }));
}

function restoreModeAData(data) {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiAContainer');
    container.innerHTML = '';
    
    data.forEach(item => {
        addABlock();
        const lastBlock = container.lastElementChild;
        lastBlock.querySelector('.refA').value = item.ref;
        lastBlock.querySelector('.valsA').value = item.vals;
    });
    
    if (data.length === 0) addABlock();
    autoCalculA();
}

// Mode C - AMÉLIORÉ AVEC FEEDBACK CURSEUR
function addCBlock() {
    const blockId = Date.now();
    const block = {
        id: blockId,
        name: `Bloc ${cBlockCounter}`,
        blockPercent: 0,
        variables: [
            { id: Date.now() + 1, name: `Variable 1`, value: 100, percent: 0 },
            { id: Date.now() + 2, name: `Variable 2`, value: 200, percent: 0 }
        ]
    };
    
    cBlocks.push(block);
    cBlockCounter++;
    renderCBlocks();
    updateCResults();
    saveAllData();
}

function renderCBlocks() {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiCContainer');
    if (cBlocks.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted); padding: 20px;">Ajoutez un premier bloc</p>';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    
    cBlocks.forEach((block) => {
        html += `
            <div class="c-block" data-block-id="${block.id}">
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" value="${block.name}" onchange="updateCBlockName(${block.id}, this.value)" 
                           style="flex:1; min-width: 150px; padding: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--bg-secondary); color: var(--text-primary);" placeholder="Nom du bloc">
                    <span style="font-weight:bold; color: var(--text-primary);">% bloc:</span>
                    <div class="slider-container">
                        <input type="range" class="c-block-slider" min="-100" max="100" value="${block.blockPercent}" 
                               oninput="updateBlockPercent(${block.id}, this.value)" onmousemove="showSliderValue(this)" ontouchstart="showSliderValue(this)" ontouchmove="showSliderValue(this)"
                               style="width: 100%;">
                        <div class="slider-value-display">${block.blockPercent}%</div>
                    </div>
                    <span style="width:50px; text-align:center; font-weight:bold; color:var(--accent-color);">${block.blockPercent}%</span>
                    <button onclick="removeCBlock(${block.id})" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>
                
                ${block.variables.map(variable => `
                    <div class="c-variable-item" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin: 8px 0; padding: 10px; background: var(--bg-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--border-light); border-radius: 5px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                        <input type="number" value="${variable.value}" style="width:100px; padding: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--bg-secondary); color: var(--text-primary);"
                               onchange="updateVariableValue(${block.id}, ${variable.id}, this.value)">
                        <span style="width:120px; text-align:left; color: var(--text-primary);">${variable.name}</span>
                        <div class="slider-container">
                            <input type="range" class="c-var-slider" min="-100" max="100" value="${variable.percent}"
                                   oninput="updateVariablePercent(${block.id}, ${variable.id}, this.value)" onmousemove="showSliderValue(this)" ontouchstart="showSliderValue(this)" ontouchmove="showSliderValue(this)"
                                   style="width: 100%;">
                            <div class="slider-value-display">${variable.percent}%</div>
                        </div>
                        <span class="c-var-value" style="min-width: 60px; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; color:${variable.percent >= 0 ? 'var(--success-color)' : 'var(--error-color)'}">
                            ${variable.percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${variable.percent}%
                        </span>
                        <button onclick="removeVariable(${block.id}, ${variable.id})" class="delete-btn" style="padding: 5px 10px;"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
                
                <button onclick="addVariableToBlock(${block.id})" class="secondary" style="width:100%; margin-top:10px; padding: 8px;">
                    <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ajouter une variable
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function showSliderValue(slider) {
    const display = slider.parentNode.querySelector('.slider-value-display');
    if (display) {
        display.textContent = slider.value + '%';
        display.style.opacity = '1';
    }
}

function updateCBlockName(blockId, newName) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) block.name = newName;
    saveAllData();
}

function updateBlockPercent(blockId, newPercent) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        let percent = parseInt(newPercent) || 0;
        if (percent > 100) percent = 100;
        if (percent < -100) percent = -100;
        
        block.blockPercent = percent;
        block.variables.forEach(variable => {
            variable.percent = percent;
        });
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function updateVariableValue(blockId, variableId, newValue) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        const variable = block.variables.find(v => v.id === variableId);
        if (variable) {
            variable.value = parseFloat(newValue) || 0;
            updateCResults();
            saveAllData();
        }
    }
}

function updateVariablePercent(blockId, variableId, newPercent) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        const variable = block.variables.find(v => v.id === variableId);
        if (variable) {
            let percent = parseInt(newPercent) || 0;
            if (percent > 100) percent = 100;
            if (percent < -100) percent = -100;
            
            variable.percent = percent;
            renderCBlocks();
            updateCResults();
            saveAllData();
        }
    }
}

function addVariableToBlock(blockId) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block) {
        block.variables.push({
            id: Date.now(),
            name: `Variable ${block.variables.length + 1}`,
            value: 100,
            percent: block.blockPercent
        });
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function removeVariable(blockId, variableId) {
    const block = cBlocks.find(b => b.id === blockId);
    if (block && block.variables.length > 1) {
        block.variables = block.variables.filter(v => v.id !== variableId);
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        saveAllData();
    } else {
        showNotification('Un bloc doit avoir au moins une variable', 'warning');
    }
}

function removeCBlock(blockId) {
    if (cBlocks.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Vous devez garder au moins un bloc', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    if (confirm('Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer ce bloc ?')) {
        cBlocks = cBlocks.filter(b => b.id !== blockId);
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
        showNotification('Bloc supprimé avec succès', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function updateCResults() {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('cResultsBody');
    const tfoot = document.getElementById('cResultsTotal');
    let html = '';
    
    let totalInitial = 0;
    let totalFinal = 0;
    let totalVariation = 0;
    
    cBlocks.forEach(block => {
        block.variables.forEach(variable => {
            const initial = variable.value;
            const final = initial * (1 + variable.percent / 100);
            const variation = final - initial;
            
            totalInitial += initial;
            totalFinal += final;
            totalVariation += variation;
            
            html += `<tr>
                <td>${block.name}</td>
                <td>${variable.name}</td>
                <td>${initial.toFixed(2)}</td>
                <td>${variable.percent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${variable.percent}%</td>
                <td>${final.toFixed(2)}</td>
                <td class="${variation >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">${variation >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${variation.toFixed(2)}</td>
            </tr>`;
        });
    });
    
    tbody.innerHTML = html;
    
    tfoot.innerHTML = `
        <tr class="total-row">
            <td colspan="2"><strong>TOTAL GLOBAL</strong></td>
            <td><strong>${totalInitial.toFixed(2)}</strong></td>
            <td></td>
            <td><strong>${totalFinal.toFixed(2)}</strong></td>
            <td class="${totalVariation >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">
                <strong>${totalVariation >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${totalVariation.toFixed(2)}</strong>
            </td>
        </tr>
    `;
    saveAllData();
}

// ============================================
// NOUVEAU MODE B : BUDGET AVANCÉ
// ============================================
function addBudgetCategory() {
    const category = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: `Catégorie ${budgetCatCount++}`,
        percent: 0,
        subs: []
    };
    
    budgetCategories.push(category);
    renderBudgetCategories();
    updateBudgetRecap();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeBudgetCategory(index) {
    if (budgetCategories.length <= 1) {
        showNotification('Vous devez garder au moins une catégorie', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    if (confirm('Supprimer cette catégorie ?')) {
        budgetCategories.splice(index, 1);
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function addBudgetSubcategory(catIndex) {
    const category = budgetCategories[catIndex];
    if (category) {
        category.subs.push({
            id: Date.now(),
            name: `Sous-catégorie ${category.subs.length + 1}`,
            percent: 0
        });
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function removeBudgetSubcategory(catIndex, subIndex) {
    const category = budgetCategories[catIndex];
    if (category && category.subs.length > 0) {
        category.subs.splice(subIndex, 1);
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function renameBudgetCategory(index) {
    const newName = prompt("Renommer la catégorie", budgetCategories[index].name);
    if (newName && newName.trim() !== '') {
        budgetCategories[index].name = newName.trim();
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function renameBudgetSubcategory(catIndex, subIndex) {
    const sub = budgetCategories[catIndex].subs[subIndex];
    if (sub) {
        const newName = prompt("Renommer la sous-catégorie", sub.name);
        if (newName && newName.trim() !== '') {
            sub.name = newName.trim();
            renderBudgetCategories();
            updateBudgetRecap();
            saveAllData();
        }
    }
}

function adjustCategoryPercent(index, value) {
    const category = budgetCategories[index];
    if (category) {
        value = parseFloat(value) || 0;
        
        // Calculer le pourcentage total actuel des autres catégories
        const otherPercent = budgetCategories.reduce((sum, cat, i) =>
            i !== index ? sum + cat.percent : sum, 0);
        
        // Limiter à 100% maximum
        const maxAllowed = 100 - otherPercent;
        category.percent = Math.min(Math.max(value, 0), maxAllowed);
        
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function adjustSubcategoryPercent(catIndex, subIndex, value) {
    const category = budgetCategories[catIndex];
    const subcategory = category.subs[subIndex];
    
    if (subcategory) {
        value = parseFloat(value) || 0;
        
        // Calculer le pourcentage total actuel des autres sous-catégories
        const otherPercent = category.subs.reduce((sum, sub, i) =>
            i !== subIndex ? sum + sub.percent : sum, 0);
        
        // Limiter à 100% maximum
        const maxAllowed = 100 - otherPercent;
        subcategory.percent = Math.min(Math.max(value, 0), maxAllowed);
        
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function renderBudgetCategories() {
    const container = document.getElementById('budgetCategories');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (budgetCategories.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-wallet fa-2x" style="margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
                <p>Ajoutez une première catégorie pour commencer</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    
    budgetCategories.forEach((cat, catIndex) => {
        html += `
            <div class="category">
                <div class="category-content">
                    <span class="editable" ondblclick="renameBudgetCategory(${catIndex})">
                        <strong>${cat.name}</strong>
                    </span>
                    <input type="number" class="budget-input" value="${cat.percent}" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" 
                        onchange="adjustCategoryPercent(${catIndex}, this.value)">
                    <span class="percent-label">%</span>
                    <button class="delete-btn" onclick="removeBudgetCategory(${catIndex})">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
        `;
        
        // Sous-catégories
        cat.subs.forEach((sub, subIndex) => {
            html += `
                <div class="subcategory">
                    <div class="subcategory-content">
                        <span class="editable" ondblclick="renameBudgetSubcategory(${catIndex}, ${subIndex})">
                            ${sub.name}
                        </span>
                        <input type="number" class="budget-input" value="${sub.percent}" min="0" max="100" step="0.01"
                            onchange="adjustSubcategoryPercent(${catIndex}, ${subIndex}, this.value)">
                        <span class="percent-label">%</span>
                        <button class="delete-btn" onclick="removeBudgetSubcategory(${catIndex}, ${subIndex})">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        });
        
        html += `
                <div style="text-align: right; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button onclick="addBudgetSubcategory(${catIndex})" class="secondary">
                        <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Sous-catégorie
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function updateBudgetRecap() {
    const budget = parseFloat(document.getElementById('budget').value) || 0;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('budgetRecap');
    if (!tbody) return;
    
    let html = '';
    let totalPercent = 0;
    
    budgetCategories.forEach(cat => {
        const catAmount = budget * cat.percent / 100;
        totalPercent += cat.percent;
        
        if (cat.subs.length === 0) {
            html += `
                <tr class="category-row">
                    <td>${cat.name}</td>
                    <td>-</td>
                    <td>${cat.percent}%</td>
                    <td>${catAmount.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                </tr>
            `;
        } else {
            html += `
                <tr class="category-row">
                    <td>${cat.name}</td>
                    <td>-</td>
                    <td>${cat.percent}%</td>
                    <td>${catAmount.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                </tr>
            `;
            
            cat.subs.forEach(sub => {
                const globalPercent = cat.percent * sub.percent / 100;
                const amount = budget * globalPercent / 100;
                
                html += `
                    <tr class="sub-row">
                        <td></td>
                        <td>${sub.name}</td>
                        <td>${globalPercent.toFixed(2)}%</td>
                        <td>${amount.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            });
        }
    });
    
    html += `
        <tr class="total-row">
            <td>TOTAL</td>
            <td>-</td>
            <td>${totalPercent}%</td>
            <td>${budget.toFixed(2)} €</td>
        </tr>
    `;
    
    tbody.innerHTML = html;
    saveAllData();
}

// ============================================
// MODE D (inchangé mais inclus pour complétude)
// ============================================
function toggleDates() {
    updateDTable();
    saveAllData();
}

function addDDate() {
    const useDates = document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked !== false;
    const id = Date.now();
    
    if (useDates) {
        const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        dDates.push({ id, date });
    } else {
        dDates.push({ id, label: `Point ${dDates.length + 1}` });
    }
    
    dSeries.forEach(s => {
        s.values[id] = 0;
    });
    
    updateDTable();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeDDate(id) {
    dDates = dDates.filter(d => d.id !== id);
    dSeries.forEach(s => {
        delete s.values[id];
    });
    updateDTable();
    saveAllData();
}

function addDSeries() {
    const newSeries = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: `Série ${dSeries.length + 1}`,
        values: {},
        color: '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16)
    };
    dDates.forEach(d => {
        newSeries.values[d.id] = 0;
    });
    dSeries.push(newSeries);
    updateDTable();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeDSeries(id) {
    dSeries = dSeries.filter(s => s.id !== id);
    updateDTable();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDDate(id, newDate) {
    const dateObj = dDates.find(d => d.id === id);
    if (dateObj) dateObj.date = newDate;
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDPointLabel(id, newLabel) {
    const pointObj = dDates.find(d => d.id === id);
    if (pointObj) pointObj.label = newLabel;
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDSeriesName(id, newName) {
    const series = dSeries.find(s => s.id === id);
    if (series) series.name = newName;
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDSeriesColor(id, newColor) {
    const series = dSeries.find(s => s.id === id);
    if (series) series.color = newColor;
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDSeriesValue(seriesId, dateId, newValue) {
    const series = dSeries.find(s => s.id === seriesId);
    if (series) series.values[dateId] = parseFloat(newValue) || 0;
    saveAllData();
}

function updateDTable() {
    const dateHeader = document.getElementById('dateHeaderRow');
    const seriesBody = document.getElementById('seriesBody');
    const useDates = document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked !== false;
    
    let headerHTML = '<th style="min-width: 160px;">Série / Date</th>';
    dDates.forEach(d => {
        if (useDates) {
            headerHTML += `
                <th>
                    <input type="date" value="${d.date || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}" onchange="updateDDate(${d.id}, this.value)" style="width:120px;">
                    <button onclick="removeDDate(${d.id})" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:11px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;">✖</button>
                </th>
            `;
        } else {
            headerHTML += `
                <th>
                    <input type="text" value="${d.label || `Point ${dDates.indexOf(d) + 1}`}" onchange="updateDPointLabel(${d.id}, this.value)" style="width:100px;">
                    <button onclick="removeDDate(${d.id})" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:11px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;">✖</button>
                </th>
            `;
        }
    });
    dateHeader.innerHTML = headerHTML;
    
    seriesBody.innerHTML = '';
    dSeries.forEach(s => {
        let row = document.createElement('tr');
        let rowHTML = `
            <td>
                <input type="text" value="${s.name}" onchange="updateDSeriesName(${s.id}, this.value)" style="width:120px;">
                <input type="color" value="${s.color}" onchange="updateDSeriesColor(${s.id}, this.value)" title="Couleur" style="width: 30px; height: 30px;">
                <button onclick="removeDSeries(${s.id})" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:11px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer;">✖</button>
            </td>
        `;
        
        dDates.forEach(d => {
            const val = s.values[d.id] || 0;
            rowHTML += `
                <td>
                    <input type="number" value="${val}" 
                           onchange="updateDSeriesValue(${s.id}, ${d.id}, this.value)" 
                           style="width:80px;" step="0.01">
                </td>
            `;
        });
        
        row.innerHTML = rowHTML;
        seriesBody.appendChild(row);
    });
}

function sortDDates() {
    const useDates = document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked !== false;
    
    if (useDates) {
        dDates.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));
    }
    updateDTable();
    saveAllData();
}

function calculerD() {
    const ctx = document.getElementById('graphD')?.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    
    if (chartD) chartD.destroy();
    
    const useDates = document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked !== false;
    let labels;
    
    if (useDates) {
        labels = dDates.map(d => d.date);
    } else {
        labels = dDates.map(d => d.label || `Point ${dDates.indexOf(d) + 1}`);
    }
    
    const datasets = dSeries.map(s => ({
        label: s.name,
        data: dDates.map(d => s.values[d.id] || 0),
        borderColor: s.color,
        backgroundColor: s.color + '20',
        fill: false,
        tension: 0.1,
        pointRadius: 5,
        pointHoverRadius: 8
    }));
    
    chartD = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: { labels, datasets },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: { position: 'top' },
                title: { display: true, text: 'Évolution temporelle' }
            },
            scales: {
                y: {
                    beginAtZero: true,
                    grid: { color: 'var(--border-light)' },
                    ticks: { color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }
                },
                x: {
                    grid: { color: 'var(--border-light)' },
                    ticks: { color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }
                }
            }
        }
    });
    saveAllData();
}

// ============================================
// MODE F - CORRIGÉ (tableau aligné)
// ============================================
function addFBlock() {
    const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
    const id = Date.now();
    
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
    div.setAttribute('data-original-value', '100');
    div.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="Nouvelle catégorie" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="100" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="color" class="colorF" value="#${Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16)}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-value="100" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
            <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(div);
    autoCalculF();
    updateSelectedCount();
    saveAllData();
}

function removeF(btn) {
    const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
    if (block) {
        block.remove();
        autoCalculF();
        updateSelectedCount();
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function autoCalculF() {
    calculerF();
    saveAllData();
}

function calculerF() {
    const ctx = document.getElementById('graphF')?.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    
    if (chartF) chartF.destroy();
    
    historiqueF = [];
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
    blocks.forEach(block => {
        historiqueF.push({
            label: block.querySelector('.labelF').value || "Sans nom",
            val: parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF').value) || 0,
            color: block.querySelector('.colorF').value || '#3498db',
            type: block.getAttribute('data-original-value') ? 'original' : 'merged'
        });
    });
    
    if (historiqueF.length === 0) {
        document.getElementById('tableauF').querySelector('tbody').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted); padding: 20px;">Aucune catégorie</td></tr>';
        document.getElementById('tableauFTotal').innerHTML = '';
        return;
    }
    
    const total = historiqueF.reduce((sum, cat) => sum + cat.val, 0);
    
    const data = {
        labels: historiqueF.map(cat => cat.label),
        datasets: [{
            data: historiqueF.map(cat => cat.val),
            backgroundColor: historiqueF.map(cat => cat.color),
            borderWidth: 2,
            borderColor: 'var(--bg-secondary)'
        }]
    };
    
    chartF = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'pie',
        data: data,
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: { position: 'right' },
                title: { display: true, text: `Répartition - Total: ${total.toFixed(2)}` },
                datalabels: {
                    formatter: (value, context) => {
                        if (total === 0) return '0%';
                        const percentage = (value / total * 100).toFixed(1);
                        return context.chart.data.labels[context.dataIndex] + '\n' + percentage + '%';
                    },
                    color: '#fff',
                    font: { weight: 'bold', size: 12 },
                    textAlign: 'center'
                }
            }
        },
        plugins: [ChartDataLabels]
    });
    
    const tbody = document.querySelector('#tableauF tbody');
    const tfoot = document.getElementById('tableauFTotal');
    
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    historiqueF.forEach(cat => {
        const percentage = total === 0 ? 0 : (cat.val / total * 100);
        const typeBadge = cat.type === 'merged' ?
            '<span style="background: #9b59b6; color: white; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px;">Fusionnée</span>' :
            '<span style="background: var(--success-color); color: white; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px;">Originale</span>';
        
        tbody.innerHTML += `<tr>
            <td><span style="display:inline-block;width:12px;height:12px;background:${cat.color};margin-right:8px;border-radius:2px;"></span>${cat.label}</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${cat.val.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${percentage.toFixed(1)}%</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${typeBadge}</td>
        </tr>`;
    });
    
    tfoot.innerHTML = `
        <tr style="font-weight: bold; background: rgba(52, 152, 219, 0.1);">
            <td colspan="2" style="text-align: right;">TOTAL</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">${total.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td style="text-align: center;">100%</td>
        </tr>
    `;
    saveAllData();
}

function toggleFusionSelection(btn) {
    const isSelected = btn.getAttribute('data-selected') === 'true';
    if (isSelected) {
        btn.textContent = '✗';
        btn.style.background = 'var(--bg-tertiary)';
        btn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
        btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
    } else {
        btn.textContent = '✓';
        btn.style.background = 'var(--success-color)';
        btn.style.color = 'white';
        btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'true');
    }
    updateSelectedCount();
    saveAllData();
}

function updateSelectedCount() {
    const selected = document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]');
    document.getElementById('selectedCount').textContent = selected.length;
}

function selectAllForFusion(select) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn').forEach(btn => {
        if (select) {
            btn.textContent = '✓';
            btn.style.background = 'var(--success-color)';
            btn.style.color = 'white';
            btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'true');
        } else {
            btn.textContent = '✗';
            btn.style.background = 'var(--bg-tertiary)';
            btn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
            btn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
        }
    });
    updateSelectedCount();
    saveAllData();
}

function mergeSelectedCategories() {
    const selected = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]'));
    if (selected.length < 2) {
        showNotification('Sélectionnez au moins 2 catégories à fusionner.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const fusionName = document.getElementById('fusionName').value || 'Fusionnée';
    let totalVal = 0;
    let color = '#3498db';
    
    const originalCategories = [];
    
    selected.forEach((btn, index) => {
        const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
        const label = block.querySelector('.labelF').value;
        const val = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.valF').value) || 0;
        totalVal += val;
        
        originalCategories.push({
            label: label,
            value: val,
            color: block.querySelector('.colorF').value,
            originalValue: block.getAttribute('data-original-value')
        });
        
        if (index === 0) color = block.querySelector('.colorF').value;
    });
    
    selected.forEach(btn => {
        const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
        block.remove();
    });
    
    const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
    const id = Date.now();
    
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
    div.setAttribute('data-original-categories', JSON.stringify(originalCategories));
    div.setAttribute('data-is-merged', 'true');
    
    div.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${fusionName}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${totalVal}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
            <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-categories='${JSON.stringify(originalCategories)}' style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
            <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top: 10px; font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">
            <i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> Fusion de ${selected.length} catégories
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(div);
    
    document.getElementById('fusionName').value = '';
    selectAllForFusion(false);
    autoCalculF();
    showNotification(`${selected.length} catégories fusionnées en "${fusionName}"`, 'success');
    saveAllData();
}

function splitSelectedCategory() {
    const selected = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.fusion-toggle-btn[data-selected="true"]'));
    if (selected.length !== 1) {
        showNotification('Sélectionnez une seule catégorie fusionnée à séparer.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const btn = selected[0];
    const block = btn.closest('.ref-block');
    const originalCategoriesJSON = block.getAttribute('data-original-categories');
    
    if (!originalCategoriesJSON) {
        showNotification('Cette catégorie n\'est pas fusionnée ou ne peut pas être séparée.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const originalCategories = JSON.parse(originalCategoriesJSON);
    
    if (confirm(`Séparer cette catégorie en ${originalCategories.length} catégories originales ?`)) {
        block.remove();
        
        const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
        
        originalCategories.forEach(cat => {
            const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
            const div = document.createElement("div");
            div.className = "ref-block";
            div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
            div.setAttribute('data-original-value', cat.originalValue || cat.value);
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${cat.label}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${cat.value}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${cat.color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-value="${cat.originalValue || cat.value}" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
                    <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
            
            container.appendChild(div);
        });
        
        selectAllForFusion(false);
        autoCalculF();
        showNotification(`Catégorie séparée en ${originalCategories.length} catégories originales`, 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function resetToOriginalValues() {
    if (confirm('Réinitialiser toutes les catégories à leurs valeurs originales ?')) {
        const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block');
        
        blocks.forEach(block => {
            const originalValue = block.getAttribute('data-original-value');
            if (originalValue) {
                const valInput = block.querySelector('.valF');
                if (valInput) {
                    valInput.value = originalValue;
                }
            }
            
            const toggleBtn = block.querySelector('.fusion-toggle-btn');
            if (toggleBtn) {
                toggleBtn.textContent = '✗';
                toggleBtn.style.background = 'var(--bg-tertiary)';
                toggleBtn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
                toggleBtn.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
            }
        });
        
        updateSelectedCount();
        autoCalculF();
        showNotification('Valeurs réinitialisées', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function saveFusionState() {
    const stateName = prompt('Nom de l\'état de fusion :', `État_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    
    if (!stateName) return;
    
    const blocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block')).map(block => ({
        label: block.querySelector('.labelF').value,
        value: block.querySelector('.valF').value,
        color: block.querySelector('.colorF').value,
        isSelected: block.querySelector('.fusion-toggle-btn').getAttribute('data-selected') === 'true',
        originalValue: block.getAttribute('data-original-value'),
        originalCategories: block.getAttribute('data-original-categories'),
        isMerged: block.getAttribute('data-is-merged') === 'true'
    }));
    
    const state = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: stateName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        blocks: blocks,
        total: historiqueF.reduce((sum, cat) => sum + cat.val, 0)
    };
    
    fusionStates.unshift(state);
    if (fusionStates.length > 10) {
        fusionStates = fusionStates.slice(0, 10);
    }
    
    localStorage.setItem(FUSION_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(fusionStates));
    loadFusionStates();
    showNotification(`État "${stateName}" sauvegardé`, 'success');
}

function loadFusionState() {
    const container = document.getElementById('fusionStatesList');
    if (container.style.display === 'none') {
        container.style.display = 'block';
        return;
    }
    
    loadFusionStates();
}

function loadFusionStates() {
    const container = document.getElementById('fusionStatesList');
    fusionStates = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(FUSION_STATES_KEY) || '[]');
    
    if (fusionStates.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Aucun état sauvegardé</div>';
        container.style.display = 'block';
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '<h5 style="margin: 0 0 10px 0;">États sauvegardés :</h5>';
    
    fusionStates.forEach(state => {
        const date = new Date(state.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
        html += `
            <div style="background: var(--bg-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 6px; padding: 10px; margin-bottom: 8px;">
