                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-weight: bold;">${state.name}</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-muted);">${date} • ${state.blocks.length} catégories</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 5px;">
                        <button onclick="restoreFusionState(${state.id})" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 12px;" class="secondary">
                            <i class="fas fa-undo"></i>
                        </button>
                        <button onclick="deleteFusionState(${state.id})" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 12px;" class="delete-btn">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
    container.style.display = 'block';
}

function restoreFusionState(stateId) {
    const state = fusionStates.find(s => s.id === stateId);
    if (!state) return;
    
    if (confirm(`Restaurer l'état "${state.name}" ?\nLes catégories actuelles seront remplacées.`)) {
        const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
        container.innerHTML = '';
        
        state.blocks.forEach(blockData => {
            const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
            const div = document.createElement("div");
            div.className = "ref-block";
            div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
            
            if (blockData.originalValue) {
                div.setAttribute('data-original-value', blockData.originalValue);
            }
            
            if (blockData.originalCategories) {
                div.setAttribute('data-original-categories', blockData.originalCategories);
            }
            
            if (blockData.isMerged) {
                div.setAttribute('data-is-merged', 'true');
            }
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${blockData.label}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${blockData.value}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${blockData.color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                    <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="${blockData.isSelected}" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none; background: ${blockData.isSelected ? 'var(--success-color)' : 'var(--bg-tertiary)'}; color: ${blockData.isSelected ? 'white' : 'var(--text-primary)'};">${blockData.isSelected ? '✓' : '✗'}</div>
                    <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
            
            container.appendChild(div);
        });
        
        updateSelectedCount();
        autoCalculF();
        document.getElementById('fusionStatesList').style.display = 'none';
        showNotification(`État "${state.name}" restauré`, 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function deleteFusionState(stateId) {
    fusionStates = fusionStates.filter(s => s.id !== stateId);
    localStorage.setItem(FUSION_STATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(fusionStates));
    loadFusionStates();
    showNotification('État supprimé', 'success');
}

function restoreModeFData(blocks) {
    const container = document.getElementById("multiFContainer");
    container.innerHTML = '';
    
    blocks.forEach(blockData => {
        const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
        const div = document.createElement("div");
        div.className = "ref-block";
        div.setAttribute('data-category-id', id);
        
        if (blockData.originalValue) {
            div.setAttribute('data-original-value', blockData.originalValue);
        }
        
        div.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <input type="text" class="labelF" placeholder="Catégorie" value="${blockData.label}" style="width:150px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                <input type="number" class="valF" placeholder="Valeur" value="${blockData.value}" style="width:100px;" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                <input type="color" class="colorF" value="${blockData.color}" oninput="autoCalculF()">
                <div class="fusion-toggle-btn" onclick="toggleFusionSelection(this)" data-selected="false" data-original-value="${blockData.originalValue || blockData.value}" style="width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 2px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-weight: bold; user-select: none;">✗</div>
                <button onclick="removeF(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
            </div>
        `;
        
        container.appendChild(div);
    });
    
    if (blocks.length === 0) {
        addFBlock();
        addFBlock();
    }
}

// Mode G - TVA
function addGBlock() {
    const container = document.getElementById("multiGContainer");
    const blockId = 'gblock-' + Date.now();
    
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "ref-block";
    div.id = blockId;
    div.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; width:100%; flex-wrap: wrap;">
            <input type="text" class="bloc-nom-input" value="Nouveau bloc" placeholder="Nom du bloc" style="flex:1; min-width: 200px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
            <label style="color: var(--text-primary); white-space: nowrap;">TVA Globale (%): <input type="number" class="global-tva-input" value="20" style="width:70px;" oninput="autoCalculG()"></label>
            <button onclick="addGLigneToBlock('${blockId}')" class="secondary"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ligne</button>
            <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
        </div>
        <div class="ligneGContainer" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px; margin-top:15px;">
            <div class="ligne-tva" style="display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <input type="text" class="libelleG" placeholder="Libellé" value="Produit" style="flex:1; min-width: 150px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <input type="number" class="valeurG" placeholder="Montant" value="100" style="width:110px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <select class="typeG" style="width:90px;" onchange="autoCalculG()">
                    <option value="HT">HT</option><option value="TTC" selected>TTC</option>
                </select>
                <input type="number" class="tvaIndividuelleG" placeholder="TVA (%)" value="20" style="width:80px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(div);
    autoCalculG();
    saveAllData();
}

function addGLigneToBlock(blockId) {
    const block = document.getElementById(blockId);
    if (!block) return;
    
    const container = block.querySelector('.ligneGContainer');
    const ligneDiv = document.createElement("div");
    ligneDiv.className = "ligne-tva";
    ligneDiv.style.cssText = "display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; flex-wrap: wrap;";
    ligneDiv.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="libelleG" placeholder="Libellé" style="flex:1; min-width: 150px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
        <input type="number" class="valeurG" placeholder="Montant" value="100" style="width:110px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
        <select class="typeG" style="width:90px;" onchange="autoCalculG()">
            <option value="HT">HT</option><option value="TTC" selected>TTC</option>
        </select>
        <input type="number" class="tvaIndividuelleG" placeholder="TVA (%)" value="20" style="width:80px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
        <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
    `;
    
    container.appendChild(ligneDiv);
    autoCalculG();
    saveAllData();
}

function autoCalculG() {
    calculerG();
    saveAllData();
}

function calculerG() {
    const resultDiv = document.getElementById("resultG");
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block');
    
    if (blocks.length === 0) {
        resultDiv.innerHTML = '<p style="color: var(--text-muted); text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Ajoutez un bloc TVA pour commencer</p>';
        return;
    }
    
    let globalHTML = "";
    
    blocks.forEach((block, blockIndex) => {
        const blocNom = block.querySelector('.bloc-nom-input').value || `Bloc ${blockIndex + 1}`;
        const globalTVA = parseFloat(block.querySelector('.global-tva-input').value) || 20;
        
        const lignes = block.querySelectorAll('.ligne-tva');
        let totalHT = 0;
        let totalTVA = 0;
        let totalTTC = 0;
        
        let tableHTML = `
            <div style="margin: 20px 0; padding: 20px; background: var(--bg-tertiary); border-radius: 10px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color);">
                <h4 style="color: var(--text-primary); margin: 0 0 15px 0;">${blocNom} (TVA par défaut: ${globalTVA}%)</h4>
                <table style="width:100%; margin-top:10px; border-collapse: collapse;">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">Libellé</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">Montant</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">Type</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TVA</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">HT</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TVA (€)</th>
                            <th style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TTC</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
        `;
        
        lignes.forEach(ligne => {
            const libelle = ligne.querySelector('.libelleG').value || "";
            const valeur = parseFloat(ligne.querySelector('.valeurG').value) || 0;
            const type = ligne.querySelector('.typeG').value;
            const tva = parseFloat(ligne.querySelector('.tvaIndividuelleG').value) || globalTVA;
            
            let ht, ttc, tvaEur;
            
            if (type === "HT") {
                ht = valeur;
                tvaEur = ht * (tva / 100);
                ttc = ht + tvaEur;
            } else {
                ttc = valeur;
                ht = ttc / (1 + (tva / 100));
                tvaEur = ttc - ht;
            }
            
            totalHT += ht;
            totalTVA += tvaEur;
            totalTTC += ttc;
            
            tableHTML += `
                <tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-light);">
                    <td style="padding: 10px;">${libelle}</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${valeur.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: center;">${type}</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${tva.toFixed(1)}%</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${ht.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${tvaEur.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                    <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right;">${ttc.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                </tr>
            `;
        });
        
        tableHTML += `
                    </tbody>
                    <tfoot style="font-weight:bold; background:rgba(52, 152, 219, 0.1);">
                        <tr>
                            <td colspan="4" style="padding: 10px; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">TOTAUX</td>
                            <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">${totalHT.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                            <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">${totalTVA.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                            <td style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);">${totalTTC.toFixed(2)} €</td>
                        </tr>
                    </tfoot>
                </table>
            </div>
        `;
        
        globalHTML += tableHTML;
    });
    
    resultDiv.innerHTML = globalHTML;
    saveAllData();
}

function collectModeGData() {
    const blocks = document.querySelectorAll('#multiGContainer .ref-block');
    return Array.from(blocks).map(block => {
        const lines = Array.from(block.querySelectorAll('.ligne-tva')).map(line => ({
            libelle: line.querySelector('.libelleG')?.value || '',
            valeur: line.querySelector('.valeurG')?.value || '0',
            type: line.querySelector('.typeG')?.value || 'HT',
            tva: line.querySelector('.tvaIndividuelleG')?.value || '20'
        }));
        
        return {
            nom: block.querySelector('.bloc-nom-input')?.value || '',
            tvaGlobale: block.querySelector('.global-tva-input')?.value || '20',
            lignes: lines
        };
    });
}

function restoreModeGData(data) {
    const container = document.getElementById('multiGContainer');
    container.innerHTML = '';
    
    data.forEach(blockData => {
        const blockId = 'gblock-' + Date.now();
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'ref-block';
        div.id = blockId;
        
        let linesHTML = '';
        blockData.lignes.forEach(line => {
            linesHTML += `
                <div class="ligne-tva" style="display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                    <input type="text" class="libelleG" placeholder="Libellé" value="${line.libelle}" style="flex:1; min-width: 150px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                    <input type="number" class="valeurG" placeholder="Montant" value="${line.valeur}" style="width:110px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                    <select class="typeG" style="width:90px;" onchange="autoCalculG()">
                        <option value="HT" ${line.type === 'HT' ? 'selected' : ''}>HT</option>
                        <option value="TTC" ${line.type === 'TTC' ? 'selected' : ''}>TTC</option>
                    </select>
                    <input type="number" class="tvaIndividuelleG" placeholder="TVA (%)" value="${line.tva}" style="width:80px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                    <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
                </div>
            `;
        });
        
        div.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; width:100%; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <input type="text" class="bloc-nom-input" value="${blockData.nom}" placeholder="Nom du bloc" style="flex:1; min-width: 200px;" oninput="autoCalculG()">
                <label style="color: var(--text-primary); white-space: nowrap;">TVA Globale (%): <input type="number" class="global-tva-input" value="${blockData.tvaGlobale}" style="width:70px;" oninput="autoCalculG()"></label>
                <button onclick="addGLigneToBlock('${blockId}')" class="secondary"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Ligne</button>
                <button onclick="removeNode(this)" class="delete-btn"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
            </div>
            <div class="ligneGContainer" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px; margin-top:15px;">
                ${linesHTML}
            </div>
        `;
        
        container.appendChild(div);
    });
    
    if (data.length === 0) addGBlock();
    autoCalculG();
}

// ============================================
// MODE H - AVEC API DE TAUX DE CHANGE EN TEMPS RÉEL
// ============================================
const currencyFlags = {
    'EUR': 'eu', 'USD': 'us', 'GBP': 'gb', 'JPY': 'jp', 'CHF': 'ch',
    'CAD': 'ca', 'AUD': 'au', 'CNY': 'cn', 'INR': 'in', 'RUB': 'ru',
    'BRL': 'br', 'KRW': 'kr', 'MXN': 'mx', 'SGD': 'sg', 'HKD': 'hk',
    'NZD': 'nz', 'SEK': 'se', 'NOK': 'no', 'DKK': 'dk', 'PLN': 'pl',
    'TRY': 'tr', 'THB': 'th', 'IDR': 'id', 'MYR': 'my', 'PHP': 'ph',
    'VND': 'vn', 'AED': 'ae', 'SAR': 'sa', 'ZAR': 'za', 'EGP': 'eg'
};

async function loadExchangeRates() {
    showNotification('Chargement des taux de change...', 'info');
    
    try {
        // D'abord, essayer de récupérer depuis le cache
        const cached = localStorage.getItem(EXCHANGE_RATES_KEY);
        const cacheDate = localStorage.getItem('exchange_rates_date');
        
        if (cached && cacheDate) {
            const cacheTime = new Date(cacheDate);
            const now = new Date();
            const hoursDiff = (now - cacheTime) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
            
            // Utiliser le cache si moins de 24 heures
            if (hoursDiff < 24) {
                exchangeRates = JSON.parse(cached);
                console.log('Taux de change chargés depuis le cache');
                updateCurrencySelects();
                convertCurrency();
                updateAllRates();
                showNotification('Taux de change chargés (cache)', 'success');
                return;
            }
        }
        
        // Si pas de cache ou cache expiré, utiliser l'API
        console.log('Récupération des taux de change depuis l\'API...');
        
        // Utiliser une API gratuite (exchangerate-api.com)
        const response = await fetch(`https://api.exchangerate-api.com/v4/latest/EUR`);
        
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`API error: ${response.status}`);
        }
        
        const data = await response.json();
        exchangeRates = data.rates;
        
        // Ajouter EUR avec taux 1
        exchangeRates['EUR'] = 1;
        
        // Sauvegarder dans le cache
        localStorage.setItem(EXCHANGE_RATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(exchangeRates));
        localStorage.setItem('exchange_rates_date', new Date().toISOString());
        
        console.log('Taux de change mis à jour depuis l\'API:', exchangeRates);
        updateCurrencySelects();
        convertCurrency();
        updateAllRates();
        showNotification('Taux de change actualisés', 'success');
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erreur chargement taux de change:', error);
        
        // Fallback: utiliser des taux par défaut si l'API échoue
        exchangeRates = {
            'EUR': 1, 'USD': 1.08, 'GBP': 0.85, 'JPY': 160, 'CHF': 0.95,
            'CAD': 1.46, 'AUD': 1.64, 'CNY': 7.78, 'INR': 89.5,
            'BRL': 5.45, 'KRW': 1445, 'MXN': 18.2, 'SGD': 1.45,
            'HKD': 8.45, 'NZD': 1.78, 'SEK': 11.2, 'NOK': 11.5,
            'DKK': 7.45, 'PLN': 4.32, 'TRY': 34.8, 'THB': 38.9,
            'IDR': 17200, 'MYR': 5.12, 'PHP': 60.8, 'VND': 27000,
            'AED': 3.97, 'SAR': 4.05, 'ZAR': 20.1, 'EGP': 50.8
        };
        
        updateCurrencySelects();
        convertCurrency();
        updateAllRates();
        showNotification('Taux de change par défaut (API indisponible)', 'warning');
    }
}

function updateCurrencySelects() {
    const fromSelect = document.getElementById('fromCurrency');
    const toSelect = document.getElementById('toCurrency');
    
    if (!fromSelect || !toSelect) return;
    
    fromSelect.innerHTML = '';
    toSelect.innerHTML = '';
    
    // Trier les devises
    const sortedCurrencies = Object.keys(exchangeRates).sort();
    
    sortedCurrencies.forEach(currency => {
        const option1 = document.createElement('option');
        option1.value = currency;
        option1.textContent = `${currency}`;
        fromSelect.appendChild(option1);
        
        const option2 = document.createElement('option');
        option2.value = currency;
        option2.textContent = `${currency}`;
        toSelect.appendChild(option2);
    });
    
    // Rétablir les dernières devises utilisées
    const lastFromCurrency = localStorage.getItem('lastFromCurrency') || 'EUR';
    const lastToCurrency = localStorage.getItem('lastToCurrency') || 'USD';
    
    if (sortedCurrencies.includes(lastFromCurrency)) {
        fromSelect.value = lastFromCurrency;
    }
    if (sortedCurrencies.includes(lastToCurrency)) {
        toSelect.value = lastToCurrency;
    }
    
    updateFlags();
}

function updateFlags() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value;
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value;
    
    const fromFlag = document.getElementById('fromFlag');
    const toFlag = document.getElementById('toFlag');
    
    if (fromCurrency && currencyFlags[fromCurrency] && fromFlag) {
        fromFlag.src = `https://flagcdn.com/w20/${currencyFlags[fromCurrency]}.png`;
        fromFlag.style.display = 'block';
    } else if (fromFlag) {
        fromFlag.src = '';
        fromFlag.style.display = 'none';
    }
    
    if (toCurrency && currencyFlags[toCurrency] && toFlag) {
        toFlag.src = `https://flagcdn.com/w20/${currencyFlags[toCurrency]}.png`;
        toFlag.style.display = 'block';
    } else if (toFlag) {
        toFlag.src = '';
        toFlag.style.display = 'none';
    }
    
    if (fromCurrency) localStorage.setItem('lastFromCurrency', fromCurrency);
    if (toCurrency) localStorage.setItem('lastToCurrency', toCurrency);
    
    convertCurrency();
}

function convertCurrency() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value;
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value;
    const fromAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('fromAmount')?.value) || 0;
    const toAmount = document.getElementById('toAmount');
    
    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency || !toAmount || !exchangeRates[fromCurrency] || !exchangeRates[toCurrency]) {
        if (toAmount) toAmount.value = '0.00';
        return;
    }
    
    try {
        const amountInEur = fromAmount / exchangeRates[fromCurrency];
        const convertedAmount = amountInEur * exchangeRates[toCurrency];
        
        toAmount.value = convertedAmount.toFixed(2);
        
        const rate = (exchangeRates[toCurrency] / exchangeRates[fromCurrency]).toFixed(4);
        const rateInfo = document.getElementById('rateInfo');
        if (rateInfo) {
            rateInfo.innerHTML = `Taux de change: 1 ${fromCurrency} = ${rate} ${toCurrency}`;
        }
        saveAllData();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erreur conversion:', error);
        if (toAmount) toAmount.value = '0.00';
    }
}

function switchCurrencies() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency');
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency');
    const fromAmount = document.getElementById('fromAmount');
    const toAmount = document.getElementById('toAmount');
    
    const tempCurrency = fromCurrency.value;
    fromCurrency.value = toCurrency.value;
    toCurrency.value = tempCurrency;
    
    const tempAmount = fromAmount.value;
    fromAmount.value = toAmount.value;
    
    updateFlags();
    convertCurrency();
}

function updateAllRates() {
    const container = document.getElementById('allRatesGrid');
    if (!container) return;
    
    let html = '';
    const baseCurrency = 'EUR';
    
    Object.entries(exchangeRates)
        .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
        .forEach(([currency, rate]) => {
            if (currency === baseCurrency) return;
            
            const flagCode = currencyFlags[currency] || 'un';
            
            html += `
                <div style="padding: 12px; background: var(--bg-secondary); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); transition: all 0.3s; cursor: pointer;" onclick="setCurrencyPair('${currency}')">
                    <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 8px;">
                        <img src="https://flagcdn.com/w20/${flagCode}.png" style="width: 24px; height: 18px; border-radius: 3px; object-fit: cover;">
                        <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-primary);">${currency}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size: 18px; color: var(--accent-color); margin-top: 5px;">${rate.toFixed(4)}</div>
                    <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">1 ${baseCurrency} = ${rate.toFixed(4)} ${currency}</div>
                </div>
            `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function setCurrencyPair(currency) {
    document.getElementById('fromCurrency').value = 'EUR';
    document.getElementById('toCurrency').value = currency;
    document.getElementById('fromAmount').value = '100';
    updateFlags();
    convertCurrency();
    showNotification(`Paire EUR/${currency} sélectionnée`, 'info');
}

function saveConversionToHistory() {
    const fromCurrency = document.getElementById('fromCurrency').value;
    const toCurrency = document.getElementById('toCurrency').value;
    const fromAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('fromAmount').value) || 0;
    const toAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('toAmount').value) || 0;
    
    if (fromAmount === 0) {
        showNotification('Entrez un montant à convertir', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    const conversion = {
        id: Date.now(),
        date: new Date().toLocaleString('fr-FR'),
        from: { currency: fromCurrency, amount: fromAmount },
        to: { currency: toCurrency, amount: toAmount },
        rate: (exchangeRates[toCurrency] / exchangeRates[fromCurrency]).toFixed(4)
    };
    
    conversionHistory.unshift(conversion);
    if (conversionHistory.length > 20) {
        conversionHistory = conversionHistory.slice(0, 20);
    }
    
    localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', JSON.stringify(conversionHistory));
    updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
    showNotification('Conversion sauvegardée', 'success');
    saveAllData();
}

function updateConversionHistoryDisplay() {
    const container = document.getElementById('conversionHistoryList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (conversionHistory.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="padding: 30px; text-align: center; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-exchange-alt" style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 10px; opacity: 0.5;"></i>
                <p>Aucune conversion dans l'historique</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    
    conversionHistory.forEach(conv => {
        html += `
            <div class="history-item" style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr auto auto; gap: 10px; padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-color); align-items: center; background: var(--bg-secondary);">
                <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${conv.date}</div>
                <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-primary);">${conv.from.amount.toFixed(2)} ${conv.from.currency}</div>
                <div style="text-align: center; color: var(--text-primary);">→</div>
                <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--success-color);">${conv.to.amount.toFixed(2)} ${conv.to.currency}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted); grid-column: 1 / -1;">Taux: ${conv.rate}</div>
                <button onclick="deleteConversion(${conv.id})" style="padding: 5px 10px; background: var(--error-color); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-size: 12px; grid-column: 4;">
                    <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function deleteConversion(id) {
    conversionHistory = conversionHistory.filter(conv => conv.id !== id);
    localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', JSON.stringify(conversionHistory));
    updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
    showNotification('Conversion supprimée', 'success');
    saveAllData();
}

function clearConversionHistory() {
    if (conversionHistory.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirm('Effacer tout l\'historique des conversions ?')) {
        conversionHistory = [];
        localStorage.setItem('conversionHistory', JSON.stringify([]));
        updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
        showNotification('Historique effacé', 'success');
        saveAllData();
    }
}

function exportHistoryToCSV() {
    if (conversionHistory.length === 0) {
        showNotification('Historique vide', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    
    let csv = 'Date;Montant Source;Devise Source;Montant Cible;Devise Cible;Taux de change\n';
    
    conversionHistory.forEach(conv => {
        csv += `${conv.date};${conv.from.amount};${conv.from.currency};${conv.to.amount};${conv.to.currency};${conv.rate}\n`;
    });
    
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', `historique_conversions_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`);
    link.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    
    showNotification('Historique exporté en CSV', 'success');
}

// ============================================
// SAUVEGARDE ET CHARGEMENT
// ============================================
function saveAllData() {
    try {
        const appData = {
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            version: '9.0',
            modeA: collectModeAData(),
            modeC: cBlocks,
            modeB: {
                categories: budgetCategories,
                budget: document.getElementById('budget')?.value || '2000',
                catCount: budgetCatCount
            },
            modeD: { dates: dDates, series: dSeries, useDates: document.getElementById('useDates')?.checked || true },
            modeF: {
                categories: historiqueF,
                blocks: Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#multiFContainer .ref-block')).map(block => ({
                    label: block.querySelector('.labelF').value,
                    value: block.querySelector('.valF').value,
                    color: block.querySelector('.colorF').value,
                    originalValue: block.getAttribute('data-original-value')
                }))
            },
            modeG: collectModeGData(),
            modeH: {
                conversions: conversionHistory,
                fromCurrency: document.getElementById('fromCurrency')?.value || 'EUR',
                toCurrency: document.getElementById('toCurrency')?.value || 'USD',
                fromAmount: document.getElementById('fromAmount')?.value || '100'
            },
            calculator: calculatorHistory,
            recentCalculations: recentCalculations,
            shortcuts: keyboardShortcuts,
            settings: settings,
            notes: notes,
            exchangeRates: exchangeRates
        };
        
        localStorage.setItem(APP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(appData));
        
        // Sauvegarder aussi les notes séparément
        localStorage.setItem(NOTES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(notes));
        
        if (settings.autoSave) {
            showNotificationAutoSave('Données sauvegardées');
        }
        
        return appData;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erreur sauvegarde:', error);
        return null;
    }
}

function loadAllData() {
    try {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(APP_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (saved) {
            const data = JSON.parse(saved);
            
            if (data.modeA) restoreModeAData(data.modeA);
            if (data.modeC) {
                cBlocks = data.modeC;
                cBlockCounter = cBlocks.length + 1;
            }
            if (data.modeB) {
                budgetCategories = data.modeB.categories || [];
                budgetCatCount = data.modeB.catCount || 1;
                if (document.getElementById('budget')) {
                    document.getElementById('budget').value = data.modeB.budget || '2000';
                }
            }
            if (data.modeD) {
                dDates = data.modeD.dates || [];
                dSeries = data.modeD.series || [];
                if (document.getElementById('useDates')) {
                    document.getElementById('useDates').checked = data.modeD.useDates !== false;
                }
            }
            if (data.modeF) {
                historiqueF = data.modeF.categories || [];
                restoreModeFData(data.modeF.blocks || []);
            }
            if (data.modeG) restoreModeGData(data.modeG);
            if (data.modeH) {
                conversionHistory = data.modeH.conversions || [];
                updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
            }
            if (data.calculator) {
                calculatorHistory = data.calculator;
            }
            if (data.recentCalculations) {
                recentCalculations = data.recentCalculations;
            }
            if (data.shortcuts) {
                keyboardShortcuts = data.shortcuts;
            }
            if (data.settings) {
                settings = { ...settings, ...data.settings };
            }
            if (data.notes) {
                notes = data.notes;
            }
            if (data.exchangeRates) {
                exchangeRates = data.exchangeRates;
            }
            
            console.log('✅ Données restaurées');
            return true;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Erreur chargement:', error);
    }
    
    initDefaultData();
    return false;
}

function initDefaultData() {
    if (cBlocks.length === 0) addCBlock();
    if (budgetCategories.length === 0) addBudgetCategory();
    if (dDates.length === 0) {
        dDates.push({ id: Date.now(), date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0] });
        dDates.push({ id: Date.now() + 1, date: new Date(Date.now() + 86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0] });
        dSeries.push({
            id: Date.now() + 2,
            name: "Série 1",
            values: {},
            color: "#" + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16)
        });
    }
    conversionHistory = [];
    calculatorHistory = [];
    recentCalculations = [];
    notes = notes || [];
}

// ============================================
// GESTION DES SAUVEGARDES (BACKUPS)
// ============================================
function showBackupModal() {
    document.getElementById('backupModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    displayBackupList();
}

function closeBackupModal() {
    document.getElementById('backupModal').classList.add('hidden');
}

function createBackup(name = null) {
    const backupData = saveAllData();
    const backupName = name || prompt('Nom de la sauvegarde :', `Sauvegarde_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    
    if (!backupName) return;
    
    const backup = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: backupName,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        data: backupData,
        size: JSON.stringify(backupData).length
    };
    
    let backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    backups.unshift(backup);
    
    if (backups.length > 10) {
        backups = backups.slice(0, 10);
    }
    
    localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
    displayBackupList();
    showNotification(`Sauvegarde "${backupName}" créée`, 'success');
}

function displayBackupList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('backupList');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    
    if (backups.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 40px 20px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <i class="fas fa-inbox fa-2x" style="margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
                <p>Aucune sauvegarde disponible</p>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    let html = '';
    backups.forEach(backup => {
        const date = new Date(backup.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
        const size = (backup.size / 1024).toFixed(2);
        
        html += `
            <div style="background: var(--bg-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 6px; padding: 15px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 5px;">${backup.name}</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">${date} • ${size} Ko</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 5px;">
                        <button onclick="restoreBackup(${backup.id})" style="padding: 8px 12px;" class="secondary">
                            <i class="fas fa-undo"></i>
                        </button>
                        <button onclick="deleteBackup(${backup.id})" style="padding: 8px 12px;" class="delete-btn">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function restoreBackup(backupId) {
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    const backup = backups.find(b => b.id === backupId);
    
    if (!backup) {
        showNotification('Sauvegarde introuvable', 'error');
        return;
    }
    
    if (confirm(`Restaurer la sauvegarde "${backup.name}" ?\nLes données actuelles seront écrasées.`)) {
        try {
            if (backup.data.modeA) restoreModeAData(backup.data.modeA);
            if (backup.data.modeC) {
                cBlocks = backup.data.modeC;
                cBlockCounter = cBlocks.length + 1;
            }
            if (backup.data.modeB) {
                budgetCategories = backup.data.modeB.categories || [];
                budgetCatCount = backup.data.modeB.catCount || 1;
                if (document.getElementById('budget')) {
                    document.getElementById('budget').value = backup.data.modeB.budget || '2000';
                }
            }
            if (backup.data.modeD) {
                dDates = backup.data.modeD.dates || [];
                dSeries = backup.data.modeD.series || [];
            }
            if (backup.data.modeF) {
                historiqueF = backup.data.modeF.categories || [];
                restoreModeFData(backup.data.modeF.blocks || []);
            }
            if (backup.data.modeG) restoreModeGData(backup.data.modeG);
            if (backup.data.modeH) {
                conversionHistory = backup.data.modeH.conversions || [];
            }
            if (backup.data.calculator) {
                calculatorHistory = backup.data.calculator;
            }
            if (backup.data.recentCalculations) {
                recentCalculations = backup.data.recentCalculations;
            }
            if (backup.data.shortcuts) {
                keyboardShortcuts = backup.data.shortcuts;
            }
            if (backup.data.settings) {
                settings = { ...settings, ...backup.data.settings };
            }
            if (backup.data.notes) {
                notes = backup.data.notes;
            }
            if (backup.data.exchangeRates) {
                exchangeRates = backup.data.exchangeRates;
            }
            
            updateAllModes();
            updateDashboard();
            renderNotes();
            showNotification(`Sauvegarde "${backup.name}" restaurée`, 'success');
            closeBackupModal();
        } catch (error) {
            showNotification('Erreur restauration', 'error');
        }
    }
}

function deleteBackup(backupId) {
    let backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    const backup = backups.find(b => b.id === backupId);
    
    if (!backup) return;
    
    if (confirm(`Supprimer la sauvegarde "${backup.name}" ?`)) {
        backups = backups.filter(b => b.id !== backupId);
        localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
        displayBackupList();
        showNotification('Sauvegarde supprimée', 'success');
    }
}

function clearAllBackups() {
    const backups = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY) || '[]');
    if (backups.length === 0) return;
    
    if (confirm(`Supprimer toutes les ${backups.length} sauvegardes ?`)) {
        localStorage.removeItem(BACKUP_STORAGE_KEY);
        displayBackupList();
        showNotification('Toutes les sauvegardes supprimées', 'success');
    }
}

function exportAllData() {
    const data = saveAllData();
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    
    const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `calculateur_sauvegarde_${dateStr}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    
    showNotification('Données exportées', 'success');
}

function importBackupFile() {
    document.getElementById('backupFileInput').click();
}

function handleBackupFileSelect(event) {
    const file = event.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(e) {
        try {
            const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
            
            if (confirm('Importer ces données ?')) {
                createBackup('Avant import');
                
                if (data.modeA) restoreModeAData(data.modeA);
                if (data.modeC) {
                    cBlocks = data.modeC;
                    cBlockCounter = cBlocks.length + 1;
                }
                if (data.modeB) {
                    budgetCategories = data.modeB.categories || [];
                    budgetCatCount = data.modeB.catCount || 1;
                }
                if (data.modeD) {
                    dDates = data.modeD.dates || [];
                    dSeries = data.modeD.series || [];
                }
                if (data.modeF) {
                    historiqueF = data.modeF.categories || [];
                    restoreModeFData(data.modeF.blocks || []);
                }
                if (data.modeG) restoreModeGData(data.modeG);
                if (data.modeH) {
                    conversionHistory = data.modeH.conversions || [];
                }
                if (data.calculator) {
                    calculatorHistory = data.calculator;
                }
                if (data.recentCalculations) {
                    recentCalculations = data.recentCalculations;
                }
                if (data.shortcuts) {
                    keyboardShortcuts = data.shortcuts;
                }
                if (data.notes) {
                    notes = data.notes;
                }
                if (data.exchangeRates) {
                    exchangeRates = data.exchangeRates;
                }
                
                updateAllModes();
                updateDashboard();
                renderNotes();
                showNotification('Données importées', 'success');
                closeBackupModal();
            }
        } catch (error) {
            showNotification('Fichier invalide', 'error');
        }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    event.target.value = '';
}

// ============================================
// INITIALISATION DES MODES
// ============================================
function initModes() {
    if (cBlocks.length === 0) addCBlock();
    if (budgetCategories.length === 0) addBudgetCategory();
    updateCResults();
    updateConversionHistoryDisplay();
    if (dDates.length > 0) updateDTable();
    if (historiqueF.length > 0) autoCalculF();
    autoCalculG();
    updateBudgetRecap();
}

function updateAllModes() {
    if (cBlocks.length > 0) {
        renderCBlocks();
        updateCResults();
    }
    if (budgetCategories.length > 0) {
        renderBudgetCategories();
        updateBudgetRecap();
    }
    if (dDates.length > 0) updateDTable();
    if (historiqueF.length > 0) autoCalculF();
    autoCalculG();
    updateDashboard();
}

console.log('✅ Calculateur Global Pro avec Widget Notes - Version Complète et Corrigée !');
</script>
</body>
</html>

        <button class="icon-btn" onclick="resetGame()">🔄</button>

        <div class="time-display" id="t1-val">10:00</div>
    </div>

<script>
    let t1, t2, activePlayer = 1, isRunning = false, timer;

    // --- SYSTÈME DE SAUVEGARDE ---
    function saveAll() {
        localStorage.setItem('chess_base', document.getElementById('base-time').value);
        localStorage.setItem('chess_inc', document.getElementById('inc-time').value);
    }

    window.onload = () => {
        // Restaurer temps
        if(localStorage.getItem('chess_base')) document.getElementById('base-time').value = localStorage.getItem('chess_base');
        if(localStorage.getItem('chess_inc')) document.getElementById('inc-time').value = localStorage.getItem('chess_inc');
        
        // Restaurer thème
        const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('chess_theme');
        if(savedTheme) {
            const opt = document.querySelector(`[data-theme="${savedTheme}"]`);
            if(opt) selectTheme(opt);
        }
    };

    function selectTheme(el) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.theme-option').forEach(o => o.classList.remove('active'));
        el.classList.add('active');
        const theme = el.getAttribute('data-theme');
        document.body.className = theme;
        localStorage.setItem('chess_theme', theme);
    }

    // --- JEU ---
    function launchGame() {
        saveAll();
        const mins = parseInt(document.getElementById('base-time').value) || 10;
        t1 = t2 = mins * 60;
        activePlayer = 1; isRunning = false;
        updateDisplay();
        document.getElementById('chess-overlay').style.display = 'flex';
        updateZones();
    }

    function togglePause() {
        isRunning = !isRunning;
        document.getElementById('pause-btn').textContent = isRunning ? "⏸" : "▶️";
        if(isRunning) {
            timer = setInterval(() => {
                if(activePlayer === 1) t1--; else t2--;
                if(t1 <= 0 || t2 <= 0) { clearInterval(timer); alert("Temps écoulé !"); }
                updateDisplay();
            }, 1000);
        } else clearInterval(timer);
    }

    function tapZone(p) {
        if(!isRunning || activePlayer !== p) return;
        const inc = parseInt(document.getElementById('inc-time').value) || 0;
        if(p === 1) { t1 += inc; activePlayer = 2; }
        else { t2 += inc; activePlayer = 1; }
        updateZones();
        updateDisplay();
    }

    function updateZones() {
        document.getElementById('p1-zone').className = `player-zone ${activePlayer === 1 ? 'active-player' : 'inactive-player'}`;
        document.getElementById('p2-zone').className = `player-zone ${activePlayer === 2 ? 'active-player' : 'inactive-player'}`;
    }

    function updateDisplay() {
        const fmt = (s) => Math.floor(s/60) + ":" + (s%60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
        document.getElementById('t1-val').textContent = fmt(t1);
        document.getElementById('t2-val').textContent = fmt(t2);
    }

    function exitGame() { clearInterval(timer); document.getElementById('chess-overlay').style.display = 'none'; }
    function resetGame() { clearInterval(timer); launchGame(); }
</script>

</body>
</html>
